Abstract: Machines that understand humans should perceive the present and anticipate the future. Existing human-centric vision model are pretrained on human images, set the state of the art in static dense perception, so motion and anticipation are out of reach. Here we present Human-JEPA, a human-centric vision model trained on video by anchored forecasting: dense targets are pinned to a frozen copy of the initialization, preventing a silent collapse of dense perception, and block masks are replaced by a pure past-to-future split, avoiding a five-point action tax and a seventeen-point re-identification collapse. Under frozen probes, Human-JEPA leads the pixel-anchored specialists on pose and person re-identification at 2.7 times fewer parameters, conceding high-resolution dense parsing, and its released predictor head is the first that does not degrade anticipation. A single safely adapted model thus serves both halves of understanding humans.

</details>


### [72] [Is Visual Prompting All You Need? Studying VLM Spatial Reasoning under Progressive Visual Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21170)
*Lars Benedikt Kaesberg,Tianyu Yang,Florian Valentin Wunderlich,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Daniel Kurzawe,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉呈现方式对视觉-语言模型（VLM）在空间推理任务中性能的影响。通过在SPaRC基准测试中引入轻量级输入侧支架，在不改变视觉模态的前提下使空间结构更易访问，结果使VLM的任务准确率提高了最多34.0个百分点，并与GRPO训练方法结合可进一步提升4.6个百分点。研究发现视觉呈现方式是决定VLM基准测试测量的是基础感知、下游推理还是两者混合的核心因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言模型在多模态推理方面进步迅速，但最近的研究表明，模型的失败往往反映了视觉定位与下游推理之间的交互作用。然而，当底层推理问题不变时，任务的视觉呈现方式如何影响模型的性能和失败模式仍不清楚。本文旨在探究视觉呈现对VLM性能的具体影响。

Method: 作者在SPaRC基准测试（基于网格的视觉空间规划基准）中引入了轻量级输入侧支架，这些支架在保持视觉模态的同时使空间结构更易于访问。他们分析了端到端任务解决和对象检测两方面，并在多个VLM模型上进行了实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，这些支架在原视觉设置下将任务准确率提高了最多34.0个百分点，并与基于GRPO的训练方法互补，相比原始视觉输入下近乎零的增益，进一步提升了4.6个百分点。分析显示这些增益与减少定位相关错误密切相关，而规则推理仍然相对具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 视觉呈现方式是决定VLM基准测试测量的是基础感知、下游推理还是两者混合的核心因素。通过优化输入表示可以显著改善模型在空间推理任务中的表现，特别是减少与视觉定位相关的错误，但规则推理仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly in multimodal reasoning, yet recent work shows that their failures often reflect an interaction between visual grounding and downstream reasoning. What remains less clear is how the visual presentation of a task shapes model performance and failure modes when the underlying reasoning problem is unchanged. We study this question in SPaRC, a benchmark for grid-based visual spatial planning, by introducing lightweight input-side scaffolds that preserve the visual modality while making spatial structure more accessible. Across multiple VLMs, these scaffolds improve task accuracy over the original visual setting by up to 34.0 percentage points and further complement GRPO-based training, yielding up to 4.6 additional accuracy points compared with near-zero gains on the original visual input. Analyses on both end-to-end task solving and object detection show that these gains are closely tied to reductions in grounding-related errors, while rule reasoning remains comparatively challenging. We find that visual presentation is a central factor that determines whether VLM benchmarks measure grounded perception, downstream reasoning, or a mixture of both.

</details>


### [73] [Towards Investigating Residual Hearing Loss: Quantification of Fibrosis in a Novel Cochlear OCT Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21189)
*Julia Dietlmeier,Benjamin Greenberg,Wenxuan He,Teresa Wilson,Rubing Xing,Jordan Hill,Adrienne Fettig,Madeline Otto,Teyhana Rounsavill,Lina A. J. Reiss,Jingang Yi,Noel E. O'Connor,George W. S. Burwood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究首次将计算机视觉技术应用于植入耳蜗的OCT数据集，通过深度学习模型（改进的UNET架构）实现对耳蜗纤维化的自动分割和定量评估，为改善人工耳蜗患者预后提供了新的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耳蜗纤维化会阻碍残余听力功能并逐渐降低电声刺激（EAS）混合人工耳蜗的疗效。需要在啮齿动物模型中客观、高分辨率、可重复地评估纤维化负担，以研究其形成机制并最终改善CI患者预后。

Method: 构建并标注慢性植入豚鼠的光学相干断层扫描（OCT）图像数据集；采用多种最先进的语义分割模型识别耳蜗纤维化及其他相关标注；与手动分割的OCT图像库进行对比验证。

Result: 改进的UNET架构（2D-OCT-UNET）在upscaled OCT输入分辨率下操作时表现最佳，能够准确识别耳蜗纤维化及相关结构；模型性能显著优于其他比较的分割方法。

Conclusion: 首次成功将计算机视觉技术应用于植入耳蜗纤维化的OCT数据集，2D-OCT-UNET模型可可靠地计算耳蜗纤维化负担，相关数据集和代码已在GitHub开源（https://github.com/juliadietlmeier/CF-OCT-segmentation），为后续研究提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Objective: Cochlear implants (CIs) are bionic prostheses that restores hearing via electrical stimulation of the auditory nerve. Hybrid CIs, which use electroacoustic stimulation (EAS), combine residual low-frequency acoustic hearing with CI electrical stimulation. Intracochlear fibrosis, which forms in response to the presence of the implant, may impede residual hearing function and gradually reduce the efficacy of EAS. It is therefore a translational objective to study the formation of cochlear fibrosis in rodents, with the goal of reducing fibrotic burden and improving outcomes for CI patients. Methods: We generate and annotate a novel dataset of optical coherence tomography (OCT) images from chronically implanted guinea pigs as part of an ongoing study focused on implant induced fibrosis. Objectively assessing fibrotic burden in this model, with high resolution and repeatability, presents an obvious use case for computer vision methods. Results: We present the results of several state-of-the-art semantic segmentation models and compare their efficacy for identifying cochlear fibrosis and other relevant annotations, using a new library of manually segmented OCT images. Conclusions: We find that the best performance is achieved by using a modified version of the well-known UNET architecture (which we term 2D-OCT-UNET) that operates on the upscaled OCT input resolution. Significance: For the first time, we have successfully applied computer vision techniques to an OCT dataset of implanted cochleae with fibrosis. Using this deep learning model, the cochlear fibrotic burden calculation can be reliably carried out as we verify in our experimental section. The dataset and the project code are available at: https://github.com/juliadietlmeier/CF-OCT-segmentation

</details>


### [74] [ES-VP : Energy-Shaped Dynamic Visual Prompting for Efficient Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21194)
*Can Jin,Ying Li,Jingchen Sun,Hongwu Peng,Jiahui Zhao,Yang Zhou,Lei Li,Dimitris N. Metaxas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Energy-Shaped Visual Prompting (ES-VP)方法，通过低秩初始化和能量引导的动态适配生成图像特定提示，在显著减少参数的同时实现了优于现有单提示和多样化提示方法的表现，建立了高效且可泛化的模型适配新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉提示方法在灵活性和效率之间存在权衡。固定提示方法忽略图像个体特征，而引入辅助网络的方法虽然能提升性能，但显著增加了参数使用量和过拟合风险，同时辅助网络与预训练模型的内在偏见限制了可扩展性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出ES-VP方法，直接利用预训练模型进行自适应提示生成，采用低秩初始化和能量引导的动态适配策略，为每张图像生成特定提示，在保证参数效率的同时提升泛化性能。

Result: 在15个数据集和5种架构上的实验表明，ES-VP持续优于当前最先进方法。例如，在CLIP架构的4个数据集上，ES-VP的平均准确率比SOTA方法DAM-VP高2.6%，同时VP参数减少了590倍。

Conclusion: ES-VP方法实现了参数效率与泛化性能的平衡，为预训练模型的高效适配建立了新的性能基准，证明了直接利用预训练模型进行自适应提示生成的有效性。

Abstract: Visual prompting (VP) has emerged as a parameter-efficient method for adapting pre-trained models to downstream tasks. However, existing approaches encounter a trade-off between flexibility and efficiency. Some methods apply a fixed prompt to all images, ignoring individual image characteristics, while others introduce auxiliary networks to generate diverse prompts. Although the latter can improve performance, it also significantly increases parameter usage and the potential for overfitting to specific datasets. Furthermore, the auxiliary networks, combined with inherent biases in pre-trained models, limit scalability and generalization. In this paper, we propose Energy-Shaped Visual Prompting (ES-VP), a novel approach that generates image-specific prompts using low-rank initialization and energy-guided dynamic adaptation, achieving superior performance with fewer parameters compared to single-prompt methods. ES-VP directly utilizes the pre-trained model for adaptive prompt generation, ensuring both parameter efficiency and improved generalization. Extensive experiments conducted on five architectures across fifteen datasets demonstrate that ES-VP consistently outperforms current state-of-the-art (SOTA) single and diverse VP methods. For instance, using the CLIP architecture across four datasets, ES-VP outperforms the SOTA method DAM-VP by an average of 2.6\% in accuracy while utilizing 590$\times$ fewer VP parameters, thereby establishing a new benchmark for efficient and generalizable model adaptation.

</details>


### [75] [Anchoring Instruction Outside Mask: Exact Reference Caching for Efficient In-Context Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21229)
*Yangshuai Liu,Zheming Li,Jiaao Li,Kang He,Ziliang Lai,Zhitai Liu,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Beyond-Mask架构，通过静态文本锚点和教师强制速度蒸馏解决多参考图像编辑中的计算效率与指令遵循冲突，实现3.92x-5.47x加速且保持全注意力生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni模态生成中，上下文条件允许扩散Transformer处理文本指令和视觉参考，但每个参考图像引入数千个token导致计算量快速增长。现有结构化稀疏注意力方法虽降低计算量，但阻止视觉参考关注文本指令，严重损害多参考编辑中的指令遵循和参考保真度。

Method: 联合重新设计token序列和注意力掩码。Beyond-Mask设计使用静态文本锚点将指令连接到参考分支，保留精确的K和V重用且不增加参数。通过教师强制速度蒸馏恢复性能损失，随后进行短策略阶段，教师监督学生访问的状态。

Result: 在三个图像编辑基准测试中，方法与全注意力生成质量匹配。使用五个参考图像时，完整40步去噪过程加速3.92倍，静态文本锚点引入可忽略的运行时开销；扩展研究中十个参考时加速达5.47倍。

Conclusion: 首次将基于策略的蒸馏用于扩散模型中的架构恢复，解决了计算效率与生成质量之间的权衡问题，实现了在保持生成质量的同时显著加速多参考图像编辑任务。

Abstract: Omnimodal generation is central to a wide range of content creation and editing applications. In-context conditioning is essential to this paradigm. It allows diffusion transformers to process text instructions and visual references in a shared attention sequence. However, each reference image introduces thousands of tokens. Computation therefore grows rapidly with the number of references. Existing methods reduce computation through structured sparse attention, which limits interactions between reference and target tokens. This structure also makes the reference K and V independent of the denoising target, allowing them to be computed once and reused across steps. However, it blocks visual references from attending to the text instruction. This substantially degrades instruction following and reference fidelity in multi-reference editing. To resolve this conflict, we jointly redesign the token sequence and attention mask. Our beyond-mask design uses static text anchors to connect the instruction to the reference branch. It preserves exact K and V reuse without adding parameters. However, this direct architectural conversion degrades generation quality. We recover the lost performance through teacher-forced velocity distillation, followed by a short on-policy stage in which the teacher supervises student-visited states. To our knowledge, this is the first use of on-policy distillation for architectural recovery in diffusion models. Across three image-editing benchmarks, our method matches full-attention generation quality. With five reference images, it accelerates the complete 40-step denoising process by 3.92x, while static text anchors introduce negligible runtime overhead; the speedup reaches 5.47x at ten references in our scaling study.

</details>


### [76] [A VLM Answer Is Not an Anomaly Score: Rank Compression in Training-Free Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21244)
*Inpyo Song,Jangwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The study demonstrates that using a probability readout (utilizing full answer distributions) significantly outperforms a generated readout (using only the most likely answer) for vision-language model (VLM)-based video anomaly detection across four benchmarks, achieving 5-13 point average gains in ranking-based metrics (AUROC/AP) due to avoidance of "generated-answer rank compression."


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs enable training-free video anomaly detection but require converting their output distributions into scalar anomaly scores for benchmark evaluation. The choice of answer interface (scale + readout rule) critically impacts the ranked scores used for AUROC/AP evaluation, yet this component is often treated as trivial formatting rather than a core detector element.

Method: The authors systematically compare two readout strategies across four 7-8B VLMs, four benchmarks, and two metrics (AUROC/AP): (1) Generated readout (using only the highest-probability answer) versus (2) Probability readout (using the full distribution over admissible answers). They analyze score resolution, rank preservation, and robustness under varying decoding strategies, prompts, and answer scales.

Result: Probability readout consistently outperformed generated readout for all benchmark-metric-VLM combinations. Average AUROC/AP gains ranged from 5 to 13 points. Generated readout suffered severe "rank compression," producing only 4-18 distinct scores despite up to 91 possible answers, while probability readout maintained finer resolution. Advantages persisted across all tested decoding strategies, prompts, and joint scoring-explanation setups.

Conclusion: The answer interface is a consequential, non-trivial component of VLM-based video anomaly detectors that directly affects evaluation outcomes. Probability readout must be preferred over generated readout, and the interface (including readout rule) should be explicitly specified and rigorously evaluated as part of the detector design.

Abstract: Vision-language models enable training-free video anomaly detection by answering questions about video segments. VAD benchmarks, however, require a scalar anomaly score for each segment and evaluate the resulting ranking using the AUROC or AP. A VLM-based detector should therefore define an answer interface: the answer scale specifies the admissible answers, and the readout rule maps the model's output distribution to a score. Because this interface can change the evaluated ranking, it is part of the detector rather than a formatting detail. The generated readout uses only the most likely answer, whereas the probability readout uses the full distribution over admissible answers. Across four 7-8B VLMs, the probability readout outperforms the generated readout for every tested combination of answer scale, benchmark, and metric, with average gains ranging from 5 to 13 points across the four benchmark-metric pairs. The gap arises because the generated readout keeps only one answer value per segment, so segment with different answer distributions can receive the same score and lose their relative order. We call this loss of relative order generated-answer rank compression. Even when the answer scale allows 91 answers, the generated readout produces only 4-18 distinct scores, whereas the probability readout retains substantially finer score resolution. The advantage persists under every decoding strategy, prompt wording, and joint scoring-explanation prompt we test. The answer interface is therefore a consequential component of VLM-based VAD and should be explicitly specified and evaluated.

</details>


### [77] [On the Transferability of Agricultural Weed Detection Under Cross-Field Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21254)
*Nikhilesh Prabhakar,Pranuthi Tenali,Wilfredo Abudeye Fernandez,Shekhar Borah,Athresh Karanam,Erik Blasch,Prabha Sundaravadivel,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了跨数据集的农业杂草检测性能，比较了无监督域自适应目标检测(DAOD)与基于相邻源数据集预训练后的少样本微调策略。研究构建了棉花杂草UAV图像数据集，发现使用仅25个标注样本的少样本微调在跨作物比较中优于无监督域自适应方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确农业中的杂草检测对于减少产量损失至关重要。现有基于UAV的方法仅在单一作物和字段中评估，缺乏模型在不同作物或字段间泛化能力的证据，导致每次新部署都需要重新标注，增加了实践成本。

Method: 研究收集并标注了棉花田杂草检测的UAV图像数据集，结合已有的大豆数据集进行评估。测试了多种跨作物检测器迁移策略，对比无监督域自适应目标检测(DAOD)与源数据集预训练后目标数据集少样本微调的方法，分析了从0到完整目标数据集的标注预算范围。

Result: 少样本微调策略使用仅25个标注目标样本即优于无监督DAOD方法，在跨作物比较中展现出更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 源域选择结合适度的目标监督比算法复杂度在迁移适应中更具成效，为减少新部署字段重新标注需求提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate agricultural weed detection in real-world field conditions is essential for precision agriculture, enabling targeted intervention and reducing yield loss. Recent work has reported strong detection performance from UAV-based imagery across a range of crops, yet existing approaches evaluate within a single crop and field, leaving practitioners with little evidence that a model trained on one crop will generalize to a new field or crop type. In this work, we characterize where cross-dataset weed-localization performance degrades and which modeling choices recover it, reducing the need to relabel every new deployment field. We introduce a newly collected and annotated UAV image dataset for agricultural weed detection in cotton fields and use it alongside an existing soybean dataset collected under a similar protocol. Using these datasets, we evaluate the performance of several strategies for transferring a detector trained on one crop to another, comparing unsupervised domain adaptive object detection (DAOD) against pretraining on a domain-adjacent source dataset followed by few-shot fine-tuning on the target dataset. Our analysis spans target-domain label budgets from zero to the full target dataset, characterizing the trade-off between adaptation strategy and annotation effort. We find that few-shot fine-tuning with as few as 25 labeled target examples outperforms unsupervised DAOD in our cross-crop comparison, suggesting that source domain selection combined with modest target supervision is more productive than algorithmic sophistication in adaptation.

</details>


### [78] [WildFin: An In-the-Wild Dataset for Fish Behavioral Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21281)
*Abigail G. Grassick,Jerome Tze-Hou Hsu,Ethan Lin,Ziang Liu,Max Whitton,Madelyn Hair,Liam Gutierrez,Haozheng Yu,Kristin Branson,Vivek Jayaraman,Michael A. Gil,Andrew M. Hein,Jennifer J. Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究团队创建了WildFin基准数据集，包含1,350小时野外工作收集的鱼类行为视频数据（9小时最终数据，超200万逐帧标签），用于评估和对比计算机视觉模型在复杂海洋环境中的表现，揭示了当前模型能力与现实需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域技术进步带来了大量野外视频数据，但专家标注成本高昂成为利用这些生态科学数据的主要瓶颈。虽然计算机视觉技术提供了潜在解决方案，但现有模型在复杂海洋环境中频繁失效，亟需系统性评估这些失败模式以推动技术改进。

Method: 构建WildFin基准数据集，涵盖两种关键现实场景：固定摄像头监控鱼群和动态潜水员跟随个体。数据集经过大规模策展，包括1,350小时野外工作和600小时专家标注，产生9小时行为数据并包含超过200万个逐帧标签。对现代视觉基础模型进行基准测试，量化静态架构与时空架构之间的权衡关系。

Result: 基准测试揭示了当前视觉模型在真实水下行为分析中的显著局限性，特别是在处理复杂海洋环境时的失败模式。研究量化了不同架构类型（静态vs时空）的性能差异和权衡关系，明确指出了模型能力与实时生态研究需求之间的差距。

Conclusion: 现有计算机视觉技术尚不足以有效支持复杂海洋环境中的鱼类行为分析，需要在模型架构和数据质量方面进行重大改进。WildFin基准为评估和推动水下行为识别技术发展提供了重要参考标准，有助于缩小技术能力与实际应用需求之间的差距。

Abstract: Recent advances in field technology have led to a massive influx of in-the-wild video data for ecological science. The primary bottleneck in leveraging this data is the high cost of expert annotation. While computer vision offers a potential solution, current models frequently fail when deployed in complex marine environments. To characterize these failures, we introduce WildFin, a novel benchmark for fish behavior recognition collected and annotated by ecologists.WildFin spans two critical real-world paradigms: stationary cameras monitoring groups of fish and dynamic divers following individual subjects. The dataset represents a massive curation effort, involving 1,350 hours of fieldwork and 600 hours of expert annotation to produce 9 hours of behavioral data with over 2 million frame-by-frame labels. We benchmark modern vision foundation models and quantify tradeoffs between static and spatiotemporal architectures, revealing the substantial gap that remains between current model capabilities and the demands of real-world underwater behavioral analysis. Project website: https://team-wildfin.github.io/.

</details>


### [79] [Difficulty-Calibrated Interpolation Paths for Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21286)
*Airin Akter Tania,Md Raihan Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出难度校准流匹配方法，通过模型自身的训练难度动态调整插值调度，在CIFAR-10等数据集上取得最佳FID分数，特别是在计算资源受限的少更新场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件流匹配中的插值调度通常预先固定且独立于数据和模型，但已知其影响收敛速度和样本质量。然而，回归难度沿路径呈系统性变化，固定调度无法适应这种变化。

Method: 提出难度校准流匹配方法：使用线性路径进行短暂预训练，记录每个时间步的损失，将调度设置为该难度曲线的分位数函数，使轨迹在速度最难学习处停留更久。该方法仅有一个超参数，保持训练目标及其梯度等价性不变，兼容无分类器引导，仅增加约2%的训练开销。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上使用相同紧凑U-Net进行受控实验，校准路径在CIFAR-10全采样预算下达到最佳FID，在大批量、少更新场景下明显优于所有固定调度，这正是计算资源最稀缺的设置。

Conclusion: 难度校准流匹配通过自适应调度策略，在不改变训练目标的前提下显著提升了生成模型性能，特别是在计算资源受限的少更新场景中，为流匹配方法提供了更高效的优化路径。

Abstract: Conditional Flow Matching trains generative models by regressing a network onto the velocity of a prescribed noise-to-data interpolation path. The interpolation schedule that shapes this path is known to affect convergence and sample quality, yet it is invariably fixed in advance, independent of both the data and the model. We show that the regression difficulty of Conditional Flow Matching varies systematically along the path, and we propose Difficulty-Calibrated Flow Matching, which derives the schedule from the model itself: a short pilot run with the linear path records the per-time loss, and the schedule is set to the quantile function of this difficulty profile, so the trajectory lingers where the velocity is hardest to learn. The method has a single hyperparameter, leaves the training objective and its gradient equivalence intact, composes with classifier-free guidance, and adds about two percent training overhead. In controlled experiments on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and Fashion-MNIST with an identical compact U-Net, the calibrated path attains the best FID on CIFAR-10 at full sampling budget and clearly outperforms all fixed schedules in the large-batch, few-update regime, precisely the setting where compute is scarcest.

</details>


### [80] [When Adaptation Hurts: Connecting Representational Drift to OOD Failures in MedSAM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21300)
*Marko Haralović,Sounic Akkaraju,Carlo Baretta,Vasil Zapryanov,Alexia Briassouli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了基于提示的医学图像分割基础模型 MedSAM 在不同分布上的泛化性能，并比较了六种适配策略（全模型微调、仅编码器 LoRA、浅/深视觉提示调优、仅解码器微调、全微调）。实验在 ISIC 2018 上训练，在 IN 和 OOD（PH2、BUSI、CBIS‑DDSM）上测试，发现适配对 IN 和近 OOD 有益，但对远 OOD 常导致性能下降。全微调在性能与效率之间取得最佳平衡，仅编码器 LoRA 是最优参数高效方案，其在远 OOD 上优于标准 LoRA 和 VPT。通过 CKA 分析发现，远 OOD 性能下降与解码器表示漂移密切相关，而编码器相似性不足以解释鲁棒性。随机 0‑100 像素抖动提示可进一步提升鲁棒性。最终结论：鲁棒的 MedSAM 适配需同时考虑提示噪声、领域偏移和表示保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 MedSAM 等基础模型在医学图像分割中展现出跨域、跨模态的零/少样本泛化能力，但其性能高度依赖于提示质量和对特定数据集的适配。缺乏对不同适配策略在 IN 和 OOD 分布上系统性比较的研究，也缺乏对鲁棒性背后表示层面机制的深入理解。

Method: 1) 在 ISIC 2018 数据集上训练 MedSAM；2) 实现并评估六种适配策略：全模型微调、仅编码器 LoRA、浅/深视觉提示调优（VPT）、仅解码器微调、全微调；3) 在 IN 和 OOD 数据集（近 OOD：PH2；远 OOD：BUSI、CBIS‑DDSM）上测试，覆盖干净与逐渐增加噪声的提示；4) 使用中心核对齐（CKA）分析编码器/解码器表示相似性与性能退化之间的关系；5) 对提示引入 0‑100 像素随机抖动，评估其对鲁棒性的影响。

Result: - 适配策略普遍提升 IN 和近 OOD 性能，但在远 OOD 上常导致性能下降。
- 全微调在性能与参数效率间取得最佳折中；仅编码器 LoRA 是最强参数高效方案，在远 OOD 上优于标准 LoRA 和 VPT。
- CKA 显示远 OOD 性能下降与解码器表示漂移高度相关，而编码器相似性单独不能解释鲁棒性。
- 提示抖动显著增强模型对提示噪声的鲁棒性，并在多个设置下提升性能。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的 MedSAM 适配需联合考虑提示噪声暴露、领域偏移和表示保持。仅编码器 LoRA 通过调整编码器以应对视觉特征的分布偏移，同时保留解码器通路，从而在远 OOD 上表现出更强的鲁棒性。提示抖动作为一种简单有效的正则化手段，可进一步提升模型的泛化与鲁棒性。未来工作可扩展至更多医学成像模态与更广泛的 OOD 场景。

Abstract: Foundation models for medical image segmentation, like prompt-based MedSAM, generalize well across domains and modalities, often in zero or few-shot setups. However, their performance depends on the quality of prompts and the adaptation of the models to custom datasets. This work systematically examines how MedSAM generalizes across diverse medical imaging benchmarks, with six adaptation strategies: full-model and encoder-only LoRA, shallow and deep visual prompt tuning (VPT), and decoder-only and full fine-tuning. Models are trained on the International Skin Imaging Collaboration Challenge (ISIC 2018) dataset and evaluated under clean and increasingly noisy prompts on IN and Out-of-Distribution (OOD) datasets: close-OOD PH2 (dermoscopy), far-OOD BUSI (Breast Ultrasound Images Dataset) and CBIS-DDSM (Curated Breast Imaging Subset of the Digital Database for Screening Mammography). We show that adaptation improves performance on IN and close-OOD data but often reduces performance on far-OOD data. Full fine-tuning provides the best tradeoff, while encoder-only LoRA is the strongest parameter-efficient alternative, outperforming standard LoRA and VPT under far-OOD shifts. Using Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA), we show that far-OOD degradation is strongly associated with drift in decoder representations, whereas encoder similarity alone does not explain robustness. This suggests encoder-only LoRA provides stronger robustness than standard LoRA by adapting the encoder to distribution shift in visual features, while preserving the decoder pathway. We further show that random 0-100 pixel jitter on prompts produces more robust and better performing models. We thus conclude that robust MedSAM adaptation requires the combined consideration of prompt noise exposure, domain shift, and representation preservation. We release our code: https://github.com/ImSounic/medsam-vpt

</details>


### [81] [Re$^3$Cap: Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning Enhancement via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21305)
*Haonan Jia,Shichao Dong,Zenghui Sun,Jiawen Zheng,Ziqi Miao,Gege Shi,Qiuyu Zhao,Jinsong Lan,Xiaoyong Zhu,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Re³Cap（检索引导图像描述精化），一种基于多模态检索的推理策略，无需额外标注即可提升图像描述质量，性能优于监督微调（SFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）在图像描述领域虽有显著进展，但在引导大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）探索新颖推理策略方面仍存在局限，导致RL与监督微调（SFT）之间存在性能差距。多模态检索可作为有效的推理信号来引导描述精化。

Method: 提出检索引导的图像描述推理策略Re³Cap，由描述精化建议器（CRS）和描述质量评估器（CQA）实例化，通过检索信号识别图像描述中的幻觉和遗漏，实现更准确详细的描述，无需额外标注。

Result: 大量实验证明该方法在图像描述任务中的优越性，甚至超越监督微调（SFT）。在COCO-LN500基准的关系推理任务上，Re³Cap相比GRPO平均提升8.64%。

Conclusion: 多模态检索可有效作为图像描述精化的推理信号。Re³Cap提供了一种新颖的图像描述增强方法，无需额外标注即可提升LVLMs的推理能力，缩小RL与SFT之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant gains in image captioning, yet it is still limited in encouraging Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) to explore novel reasoning strategies. This limitation leads to a performance gap between RL and Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT). In this paper, we argue that multi-modal retrieval can serve as an effective reasoning signal for caption refinement. Based on this insight, we present the Retrieval-Guided Refinement for Image Captioning (Re$^3$Cap), a retrieval-guided reasoning strategy that enhances image captioning without requiring additional annotations. Instantiated by Caption Refinement Suggester (CRS) and Caption Quality Assessor (CQA), this strategy identifies hallucinations and omissions in image captions, leading to more accurate and detailed descriptions. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our method in image captioning, even compared with Supervised Fine-Tuning. Especially, Re$^3$Cap outperforms GRPO with an average improvement of 8.64% in relation reasoning on the COCO-LN500 benchmark.

</details>


### [82] [OmniAssistBench: Assistant-style Interaction Benchmark for Omni-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21360)
*Xianyun Sun,Chaoyou Fu,Zhengye Zhang,Feiyang Duan,Qingyuan Cao,Yonghui Niu,Sihang Yuan,Ge Zhang,Caifeng Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniAssistBench基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型作为实时视频助手的交互能力。数据集通过逆向工程现有网络视频构建，模拟多轮交互场景。结果显示Gemini-3-Pro得66.4分，Qwen3-Omni-Instruct得51.2分（满分100），当前模型在视觉提示理解、多轮上下文保持和响应时机方面仍存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多模态大语言模型作为实时视频助手面临重大挑战：模型的不可预测响应会动态改变用户后续行为，静态离线数据集无法适配这种动态交互；同时真实交互视频数据稀缺。

Method: 通过逆向工程现有互联网视频构建数据集：从源视频中推导逻辑用户目标，将视频分割为多轮片段模拟连续交互；为处理相同目标可通过不同路径实现的问题，提供源自视频的预定义先验知识，要求模型引导用户沿相同路线操作；整个流程耗时超过1000专家人时。

Result: 专有的Gemini-3-Pro达到66.4分（满分100），开源的Qwen3-Omni-Instruct达到51.2分。当前模型通常能理解用户输入，但经常提供错误或不完整的答案，具体表现为：难以处理视觉提示（如手势）、多轮交互中无法维持历史上下文、无法延迟响应直至目标事件发生。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型在成为可靠交互助手前仍有巨大提升空间，特别是在视觉理解、上下文维持和时机把握方面需要显著改进。

Abstract: Recent omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) show great potential as real-time video assistants, which continuously perceive environments and guide users to achieve specific goals. Unlike traditional passive video understanding, interactive assistants should actively combine visual states, user goals, and prior knowledge to provide effective help. Evaluating this is rather challenging, as the model's unpredictable response dynamically changes the user's subsequent actions, which static offline datasets cannot accommodate. To address this bottleneck, we introduce OmniAssistBench. To solve the issue of diverging interaction paths where the same user goal can be achieved through various methods, we provide models with predefined priors derived from the source video, requiring them to guide users along the exact same routes. Since real interaction videos are rare, we construct the dataset by reverse-engineering existing Internet videos. We deduce logical user goals and segment the videos into multi-turn clips to simulate continuous interactions. This rigorous pipeline required over 1000 expert person-hours to build the dataset. Results show that the proprietary Gemini-3-Pro reaches 66.4 out of the max point of 100, while the open-source Qwen3-Omni-Instruct achieves 51.2. Although current models generally understand user inputs, they frequently provide incorrect or incomplete answers. Specifically, they struggle with visual prompts (e.g., hand gestures), fail to maintain historical context during multi-turn interactions, and fail to delay response until the target event. Results indicate substantial room for improvement before models can become reliable assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Beyond Raw Transcripts: Structured Persona Extraction for LLM-Based Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20344)
*Iris Ye,Tianze Deng,Ozan Candogan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于大语言模型的"数字孪生"技术，发现关键瓶颈不在于信息量而在于人格信息的组织方式，并提出了一种自动结构发现流程来优化不同任务下的人格表示结构，实验证明该方法能显著提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注信息压缩对数字孪生性能的影响，但发现压缩长转录本为摘要并不会显著降低预测准确性，表明信息量并非主要瓶颈。作者认为真正的限制在于人格信息在提供给模拟器模型前的组织方式，即结构问题而非数量问题。

Method: 1. 基于消费者行为理论设计手工模式BDE（背景、决策程序、评估），将非结构化摘要与结构化人格表示进行对比；2. 在同质基准Twin-2K-500上评估，并用gpt-5.4-mini和Qwen3-8B进行鲁棒性验证；3. 开发自动结构发现流程，让LLM迭代提出并优化任务特定的人格结构和提取提示；4. 在包含13个异质性领域的基准上全面测试该方法的有效性。

Result: 手工BDE模式在同质基准上比原始转录本提高1.91个百分点准确率，但在异质任务中性能退化为与基线无差异；自动结构发现流程在13个多样性子研究基准上平均准确率提升1.91个百分点，成功消除了固定模式在异质任务上的性能损失，展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLM数字孪生的核心约束在于信息结构而非信息量，最优的人格表示结构具有任务依赖性，自适应的结构发现机制是解决跨任务泛化问题的有效途径。

Abstract: LLM-based "digital twins" aim to simulate how an individual would behavein new environments or respond to novel questions, given some representation of that individual's prior responses. A common approach constructs this representation from survey transcripts or summaries responses. Prior work shows that compressing long transcripts into shorter LLM-generated summaries does not significantly reduce predictive accuracy, suggesting that information volume is not the primary bottleneck.
  In this work, we argue that the key limitation is instead structural:how persona information is organized before being provided to thesimulator model. We study this by comparing unstructured summaries with structured persona representations. First, we introduce a hand-craftedschema (BDE: Background, Decision procedure, Evaluation), grounded in consumer-behavior theory, and show that it improves predictive accuracy over raw transcripts by +1.91 percentage points on a homogeneous benchmark (Twin-2K-500), with similar gains on gpt-5.4-mini and Qwen3-8B as robustness checks. However, this fixed structure does not generalizeacross more heterogeneous tasks, where performance is statistically indistinguishable from the raw transcript baseline.
  To address this limitation, we propose an automatic structure-discovery pipeline in which an LLM iteratively proposes and refines task-specific persona structures and extraction prompts. On a benchmark of 13 diverse sub-studies, this approach restores performance, improving mean accuracy by +1.91 percentage points over the raw transcript baseline and eliminating significant losses observed with the fixed schema.
  Overall, our results suggest that the main constraint in LLM-based digital twins is not how much information is provided, but how it is structured -- and that the optimal structure depends on the task.

</details>


### [84] [When Vocabulary Comprehension Fails Clinical Reasoning: Evaluating Therapy Bots' Safety Risks for Generation Alpha](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20345)
*Manisha Mehta,Virendra Mehta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对Z世代（Gen Alpha）心理健康对话模式的AI系统存在显著安全漏洞：主流LLM虽能理解76-82%的专业词汇，但临床风险校准准确率仅64-72%，产生10-14个百分点的认知鸿沟（人类治疗师仅3pp）。6种典型失效模式导致34%基础误判率，年增约14.7万未识别危机案例，现有轻量级干预措施无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国540万青少年（占13.1%）依赖生成式AI进行心理健康咨询，但青少年特有的语言特征（如夸张表达、语义漂移、多义性）未被现有系统验证。多起因AI聊天机器人交互导致的青少年死亡事件凸显亟需系统性评估，而当前疗法应用和通用聊天机器人均缺乏针对Z世代沟通模式的 validated 安全协议。

Method: 构建双基准测试体系：(1) 64个经母语者（ICC=0.72）和临床医生（kappa=0.78）验证的Z世代心理健康表达样本；(2) 75组多轮对话（780轮次），包含标准版与Z世代版配对语料。对Claude、GPT-4o、Llama-3.1等主流LLM架构进行词汇理解度、临床风险校准能力测试，并分析失效模式及干预措施效果。

Result: 1) 所有模型存在10-14pp词汇理解与风险校准差距（p<.001, d>0.48），人类治疗师无显著差距(3pp, p=.22)；2) 差距随模糊度增加扩大（7pp→18pp）；3) 识别6种复合失效模式（讽刺掩盖29pp、风险最小化接受43pp等），≥3种叠加时误判率达94%；4) 轻量级缓解措施失效，仅重型脚手架方案达人类水平但成本翻倍(6.4x)；5) 34%基础误判率估算年致146,880起未识别危机事件。

Conclusion: 必须实施强制人机协同架构、季度青少年专用验证、性能透明披露及监管框架。技术层面需开发青年专属语义模型，政策层面应禁止未经验证的AI直接提供心理健康干预服务，伦理层面需建立跨学科安全认证体系以应对Z世代特有的语言-风险关联复杂性。

Abstract: Conversational AI systems have become informal mental health support resources for Generation Alpha (Gen Alpha, born 2010-2024), with 13.1% of U.S. adolescents (5.4 million) using generative AI for mental health advice. While these systems, from therapy apps to general chatbots, rely on large language models trained on extensive psychological literature, their safety for youth communication patterns characterized by hyperbolic language, ironic positivity, rapid semantic drift, and contextual polysemy remains unvalidated. Following multiple adolescent deaths linked to AI chatbot interactions, systematic evaluation is critical. We present two benchmarks: (1) 64 Gen Alpha mental health expressions validated by native speakers (ICC=0.72) and clinicians (kappa=0.78); (2) 75 multi-turn conversations (780 turns) with paired Standard/Gen Alpha versions. Across evaluations of LLM architectures underlying therapy apps and general chatbots - Claude, GPT-4o, Llama-3.1 - models understand 76-82% of vocabulary but correctly calibrate only 64-72% of clinical risk, creating a 10-14 percentage point (pp) vocabulary-comprehension gap (p<.001, d>0.48) absent in human therapists (3pp, p=.22). The gap is architecturally consistent and widens with ambiguity (7pp -> 18pp). We identify six failure patterns: sarcasm masking (29pp), minimization acceptance (43pp), informal style bias (24pp), risk-stratified ambiguity (19pp), semantic drift (19pp), context-dependent violence (7pp). Patterns compound; three or more yield 94% miss rates. Lightweight mitigations fail; only heavy scaffolding achieves human performance (6.4x cost). With 34% baseline miss rate yielding 146,880 estimated annual missed crises, we recommend mandatory human-in-the-loop architectures, quarterly youth-specific validation, transparent performance disclosure, and regulatory frameworks for youth-facing mental health AI.

</details>


### [85] [Building and Evaluating a Synthetic Bengali Speech Resource for Telecom Customer Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20346)
*Kawshik Kumar Paul,Md. Nafiul Alam Fuji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文创建并公开了一个用于电信客服场景的合成孟加拉语语音数据集，包含10,000个音频-文本对（约26.82小时），使用OmniVoice语音克隆技术生成，评估显示词错误率2.54%，字符错误率0.59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向客户的语音系统通常需要特定领域的语言覆盖。针对孟加拉语在电信客服场景的应用需求，需要创建高质量、大规模的合成语音数据集来支持语音识别和语音合成等研究。

Method: 使用OmniVoice语音克隆模式，基于真实女性参考录音和转录文本来合成语音，参数包括bfloat16精度、16步扩散采样、语速控制值1.0。数据集分为训练集9,000条、验证集500条、测试集500条，并在Hugging Face上以CC-BY-4.0许可公开。同时提供原始孟加拉语文本和用于ASR/STT训练评估的规范化转录文本。评估采用基于bengaliAI/tugstugi_bengaliai-regional-asr_whisper-medium微调的领域适应Whisper ASR模型进行自动评估，并对部分样本进行人工听测。

Result: 在全部10,000个样本上，平均词错误率(WER)为2.54%，平均字符错误率(CER)为0.59%，中位数WER和CER均为0.00%。这些结果显示出在选定的自动评估流程下文本-音频一致性较强。

Conclusion: 该数据集为孟加拉语语音处理研究提供了有价值的资源，低WER/CER表明合成语音质量较高。论文也讨论了合成语音的局限性以及基于STT评估方法的不足。数据集对推动孟加拉语语音技术发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: Speech systems used in customer-facing applications often require domain-specific language coverage. We present a synthetic Bengali speech dataset for telecom customer-care scenarios. The dataset contains 10,000 audio-text pairs, approximately 26.82 hours of 24 kHz speech, and predefined train, validation, and test splits of 9,000, 500, and 500 examples. It is publicly released on Hugging Face under the CC-BY-4.0 license. The speech was generated with OmniVoice in voice-cloning mode using a real female reference recording and transcript, with bfloat16 precision, 16 diffusion sampling steps, and a speaking-rate control value of 1.0. Along with the original Bengali text, the dataset provides a normalized transcript field designed for ASR/STT training and evaluation. We report an automatic intelligibility check over all 10,000 samples using a domain-adapted Whisper ASR model fine-tuned from bengaliAI/tugstugi_bengaliai-regional-asr_whisper-medium, along with a manual listening check on selected samples. The evaluation gives an average WER of 2.54%, an average CER of 0.59%, and median WER and CER values of 0.00%. These results suggest strong text-audio consistency under the selected automatic evaluation pipeline, while the paper also discusses the limitations of synthetic speech and STT-based evaluation.

</details>


### [86] [Who Do Language Models Think Is Competent? A Mechanistic Analysis of Occupational Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20347)
*Keren Fuentes,Aaron Mueller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了语言模型内部表征中存在的隐性偏见，即使行为偏见评估显示模型无偏见，其内部表征仍可能包含基于人口统计特征的偏见关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型虽然通过行为偏见评估，但尚不清楚它们是真的不再包含偏见关联，还是仅仅学会了不表达偏见。研究旨在探究模型内部表征层面是否存在未被发现的偏见。

Method: 提出因果框架将职业偏见分解为两个测量点：模型对用户能力的内部表征和可观察输出；推导用户专业能力的表征引导向量；在问答任务和招聘任务中验证其因果中介作用；将框架应用于多个开放权重模型。

Result: 发现性别、种族和社会经济地位等人口统计属性影响模型对用户专业能力的表征，即使行为指标未检测到群体间差异；这些表征在干预下能影响下游行为，表明存在仅靠行为指标无法检测的失败模式。

Conclusion: 语言模型的行为偏见评估不足以完全反映其偏见状况，内部表征偏见可能导致潜在的风险，需要更深入的方法来检测和消除模型中的隐性偏见。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) often pass behavioral bias evaluations, but it remains unclear whether they no longer represent the underlying associations that give rise to biases, or have merely learned not to express them. In this study, we show that representational biases are often detectable, even when behavioral biases are not visible. We introduce a causal framework that decomposes occupational bias into two measurement points: a model's internal representation of a user's competence, and its observable outputs. We derive steering vectors for representations of user expertise, and verify that they causally mediate model behavior in both a question-answering task and a hiring task. Applying this framework to several open-weight models, we find that demographic attributes, such as gender, race, and socioeconomic status, influence a model's representation of user expertise, even in cases where behavioral metrics detect no disparity between demographics. We show that these model representations can influence downstream behavior under intervention, suggesting failure modes that behavioral metrics alone may not detect.

</details>


### [87] [Inhibitory Attention for Clinical Long-Context Reasoning: Characterizing and Mitigating Lost-in-the-Middle Effects in EHR Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20348)
*Sanjay Basu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Electronic health records now routinely exceed 100,000 tokens per patient. Yet large language models exhibit the lost-in-the-middle (LitM) effect: information near the center of a long context is retrieved less reliably than information near the edges. In clinical use this is not benign: the single most consequential fact in a note can sit at its center. We term this the clinical lost-in-the-middle (CLitM) problem, give its first systematic characterization using MedAlign, and compare context-selection strategies as remedies. Across 2,196 instruction-response pairs and six language models, we observe a 21.9 percentage-point gap between peak accuracy (59.5%, 95% CI [46.3, 71.0], 20-30% decile) and trough accuracy (37.6% [23.2, 52.5] at 70-80%); 67.8% of reference answers fall between the 10th and 90th percentiles of the EHR timeline, inside the CLitM trough. We introduce Query-Conditioned Clinical Suppression (QCCS), a lightweight query-conditioned selection gate, and evaluate it against BM25, BM25 with section-header filtering, dense retrieval, and cross-encoder reranking (N=83 held-out instructions). With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (16k context), QCCS outperforms all five comparators under LLM-as-judge scoring: for middle-position instructions QCCS reaches 16.7% versus BM25 3.3%, cross-encoder 0.0%, dense 0.0%, and full context 6.7%; overall QCCS reaches 25.3% versus at most 3.6% for retrieval-only comparators. This advantage is not explained by retrieval recall: at k=20, BM25 retrieves the gold evidence sentence in 98.8% of instructions (QCCS 34.9%), yet retrieval arms stay at most 2.6% accurate even when they retrieve it, whereas QCCS reaches 25.0% even when it does not. In this proof-of-concept evaluation, query-aligned context selection predicts EHR instruction-following accuracy better than gold-sentence retrieval recall.

</details>


### [88] [Beyond Prompt Engineering: A Systematic Analysis of Prompt Lexical Sensitivity and Its Impacts on Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20349)
*Qipeng Xie,Zi Liang,Jiafei Wu,Yufei Chen,Weizheng Wang,Wenao Ma,Zhong Ming,Haiqin Yang,Kaishun Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次通过132万级提示变体的n-gram级机制分析，揭示大语言模型提示性能稳定性遵循"规模律"——平均性能越高则鲁棒性越强。发现领域术语和显式动作指令是两大核心稳定因子，并开发自动化提示优化Agent，在代码生成任务中实现40.7%性能方差降低且不损失均值性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示工程依赖黑盒优化和粗糙模板，缺乏对大语言模型表面级提示变异的系统性解释，导致微调困难且结果不可靠。

Method: 构建13.2万个提示变体数据集进行n-gram级机制分析，识别影响性能稳定性的语言学驱动因素；基于发现设计自动化Prompt-Refining Agent，通过注入领域锚定词和操作约束重构提示。

Result: 发现提示性能稳定性与平均任务性能呈正相关；领域术语和显式动作指令能约束模型解释空间；优化后代码生成任务性能方差降低40.7%，均值性能保持或提升。

Conclusion: 提出统计可验证且机制可解释的提示工程框架，证实通过结构化语言要素可系统性提升大模型鲁棒性，为可靠提示工程提供理论基础与实践工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit extreme sensitivity to surface-level prompt variations, in which minor lexical changes can trigger disproportionate performance fluctuations. Moving beyond black-box optimization and coarse-grained templates, we present the first large-scale, n-gram token-level mechanistic analysis of prompt stability, leveraging a dataset of 132,000 prompt variants. Our investigation reveals a fundamental Scaling Law of Prompt Performance Stability: higher average task performance is strongly associated with lower variance and greater robustness across prompt perturbation. We identify two core linguistic drivers underlying this robustness: (1) Domain-Specific Terminology, which tightly anchors semantic boundaries, and (2) Explicit Action Directives, which formalize reasoning trajectories. Together, these elements constrain the model's interpretative space, effectively ``locking in'' more deterministic generation behavior. Building on these insights, we introduce an automated Prompt-Refining Agent that systematically restructures input queries by injecting domain anchoring and operational constraints. Empirical evaluation shows that our approach reduces performance variance by 40.7% in code generation task, while preserving or improving mean performance. These findings provide a statistically grounded and mechanistically interpretable framework for achieving robust prompt engineering.

</details>


### [89] [Free-Text Evaluation of LLMs for 5G Domain Knowledge and Fault Analysis using LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21021)
*Rishiraj Sengupta,Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mohammad Shojafar,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了三款轻量级LLM（Claude-Haiku-4.5、GPT-5.4-Mini、Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite）在5G领域自由文本故障诊断任务中的性能，发现所有模型故障诊断准确率均≥90%，但3GPP/O-RAN规范零样本召回率低于60%，且Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在推理成本与延迟方面表现最优，最适合生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的5G及新兴6G网络故障分析需要领域专业知识来解析自由文本诊断（包括根本原因解释和推荐操作）。现有基准测试依赖受限的单选题（MCQ）和固定答案键，无法评估轻量级、边缘可部署模型在开放式自由文本诊断推理中的能力。因此，需要评估轻量级边缘可部署AI模型在开放式诊断推理中的表现，并建立可靠的框架来大规模验证这些文本输出。

Method: 在三个基准测试（TeleQNA ORAN FT、5G-Faults FT、TeleInter FT）上，以自由文本生成格式评估三款轻量级LLM的5G领域知识和故障分析能力。采用三名独立前沿评审者对输出进行评分，并测量成对评审者间一致性作为LLM-as-Judge方法的实证测试。

Result: 所有三个模型在故障诊断中均达到至少90%的准确率；零样本召回3GPP和O-RAN规范仍是关键差距，所有模型得分均低于60%；所有运行中平均评审者间一致性至少为0.90，表明多评审者LLM评分能够为开放式电信响应产生一致、可复现的评分；从效率来看，Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite提供了最佳的效率权衡，结合了有竞争力的准确率和最低的推理成本与延迟。

Conclusion: 多评审者LLM评分方法能够为开放式电信响应产生一致、可复现的评分，验证了该方法的可靠性。轻量级LLM在自由文本5G故障诊断中表现出色（准确率≥90%），但在3GPP/O-RAN规范零样本召回方面存在显著差距（<60%）。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite因其优异的推理成本、延迟和准确率平衡，成为生产电信部署的最适合候选模型。未来工作应聚焦于提升轻量级LLM对电信规范的零样本召回能力。

Abstract: Real-world fault analysis in 5G and emerging 6G networks demands domain expertise to analyze free-text diagnostics, including root-cause explanations and recommended actions. LLMs have emerged as a promising approach to automating this, yet whether lightweight, edge-deployable models are capable of performing in-depth free-text diagnostics remains an open question. While existing benchmarks rely on restrictive MCQs with fixed answer keys, this paper evaluates 5G domain understanding and fault analysis in a free-text generation format. Transitioning to this paradigm requires evaluating lightweight, edge-deployable AI models on open-ended diagnostic reasoning, alongside a dependable framework to validate these text outputs at scale. To address this we evaluate three lightweight LLMs, Claude-Haiku-4.5, GPT-5.4-Mini, and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite, on free-text 5G domain knowledge and fault-analysis tasks across three benchmarks, TeleQNA ORAN FT, 5G-Faults FT, and TeleInter FT. Three independent frontier judges score outputs, and pairwise inter-judge agreement is measured as an empirical test of the LLM-as-Judge methodology. All three models reach at least 90% accuracy on fault diagnosis, while zero-shot recall of 3GPP and O-RAN specifications remains the critical gap, with all models scoring below 60%. Mean inter-judge agreement is at least 0.90 across all runs, indicating that multi-judge LLM scoring produces consistent, reproducible grades for open-ended telecom responses. Operationally, Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite offers the best efficiency trade-off, combining competitive accuracy with the lowest inference cost and latency, making it the most suitable candidate for production telecom deployments.

</details>


### [90] [How to Train a Real-World Silicon Concierge? Internalizing Complex Business Workflow to Only OneModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20350)
*Chang Liu,Chaoyang Ning,Dayi Jiang,Enrui Gu,Fang Ran,Hongyan Xue,Huaqing Li,Hui Cai,Jia Liu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Jiawei Luo,Jin Zhou,Leshen Zhu,Lihui Chen,Liying Ma,Lyuxin Xue,Mengjian Ji,Ruijia Xu,Wei Ren,Wei Wu,Xiaoling Qu,Xiaoyun Feng,Xin Zhang,Xixie Zhou,Xuanwei Hu,Yan Chen,Yichao Wang,Yongqi Tong,Yu Liu,Yuhong Zhou,Zemin Sun,Zhenwen Xu,Zhiling Liu,Zifan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出OneModel架构，通过将业务逻辑直接内化到模型参数中，替代传统模块化流水线架构，实现延迟降低50%以上，智能解决率从64.3%提升至83.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工业智能体依赖模块化流水线（Router、Retriever、Planner、Executor、Responder、Reviewer等组件），这些系统容易分裂为临时修补的迷宫，导致级联错误和高延迟问题。

Method: OneModel采用持续预训练（CPT）和逻辑编译SFT技术，将碎片化的业务规则转化为统一注意力空间内的模型推理能力，把复杂业务逻辑和标准操作流程直接内化到模型参数中。

Result: 在金融服务系统部署中，A/B测试显示端到端延迟从18.7秒降至8.0秒（降低超50%），智能解决率从64.3%提升至83.3%，有效突破了延迟、准确性和复杂性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: OneModel能够用内化的认知直觉替代脆弱的工程逻辑，为工业智能体从复杂易错的流程向统一模型架构过渡提供了可扩展的蓝图。

Abstract: Traditional industrial agents rely on modular pipelines, including Router, Retriever, Planner, Executor, Responder, Reviewer, and other components. These systems often fracture into a labyrinth of ad-hoc patches, leading to cascading errors and high latency. We propose OneModel, an applicable paradigm shift from external workflows to internalized knowledge representation. Unlike modular systems that slice fluid user intents into static steps, OneModel consolidates complex business logic and SOPs directly into the model parameters. Through Continual Pre-training (CPT) and logic-compilation SFT, we transform fragmented business rules into intuitive model reasoning within a unified attention space. Deployed in our global financial service system, OneModel effectively breaks the trade-off between latency, accuracy, and complexity. Online A/B testing demonstrates an end-to-end latency reduction of more than 50 percent, from 18.7 seconds to 8.0 seconds, while the Intelligent Resolution Rate (IRR) increases from 64.3 percent to 83.3 percent. The results show that OneModel can replace brittle engineering logic with internalized cognitive intuition, offering a scalable blueprint for transitioning industrial agents from complex, error-prone workflows to unified model architectures.

</details>


### [91] [Exploratory As-Analyzed No-Detection of Culturally-Marked Predicate-Triggered PII Amplification in a Synthetic-English RAG Probe: A Predicate-Resource-Confounded Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20351)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 未检测到文化标记查询比中性查询导致更多PII泄露，但受方法学局限（确认性检验未执行、名称泄露指标受提示回显污染）影响，结论为"未检出"而非"无效应"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究针对文化标记人群（英裔/拉丁美洲西语/阿拉伯/印地语群体）的刻板印象负载查询是否比等效中性查询从RAG系统中泄露更多个人身份信息（PII），以评估AI系统对不同文化群体的隐私风险差异。

Method: 预注册四文化审计（en-Anglo/es-LATAM/Arabic/Hindi），基于合成英文PII语料库，比较五组查询构型（Stereotype-Trigger Leakage Delta, STLD）。关键限制：1) 锁定确认性估计器未运行，所有检验均为探索性或敏感性分析；2) 名称泄露指标受提示回显伪影污染（模型直接复述提问中的姓名）。仅分析较可靠的渠道：邮箱、电话、SSN类似字段、地址。

Result: 经多重比较校正后，在四种文化中均未发现刻板印象驱动的信息泄露放大效应（基于邮件/电话/SSN/地址渠道）。但样本量仅能检测中等效应，且文化标记探针混淆了刻板印象内容与文化标志/传统实践。

Conclusion: 研究结果为"未检出文化标记谓词泄露"，而非"证明无文化标记谓词泄露效应"。原因包括：1) 确认性分析缺失；2) 样本功效不足；3) 文化标记探针存在本质性混淆（刻板印象内容与文化特征耦合），导致无法明确归因。

Abstract: We ask whether stereotype-loaded queries about culturally marked people leak more personal information from a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system than otherwise-equivalent neutral queries. We pre-register a four-culture audit (en-Anglo, es-LATAM, Arabic, Hindi) on a synthetic English PII corpus, comparing five query arms we call the Stereotype-Trigger Leakage Delta (STLD). Two caveats up front. Our locked confirmatory estimator was never run, so every test in the paper is exploratory or sensitivity, with all plan deviations listed in the appendix. And the name-leakage metric is contaminated by a prompt-echo artifact: the model often just re-emits the name we asked about, which inflates apparent leakage without any retrieval at all. On the cleaner channels (email, phone, ssn-like, address), we find no stereotype-driven amplification on any of the four cultures after multiple-comparison correction. Because our sample is only powered for mid-sized effects, and because the culturally marked probes mix stereotype content with cultural markers and heritage practices, we present this as no detection, not evidence of no effect, of culturally marked predicate leakage that is confounded with the underlying resource.

</details>


### [92] [The Divergence Hypothesis: Unmasking Lexical Interference and Label Bias in Mental Health NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20353)
*Moustafa Yehia Hassan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TSS（Triple-Stream Stress probe）是一个多通道诊断框架，揭示了计算心理健康分类器在人类标注与自动标注数据上学习不同语言捷径的问题，通过三通道分解和DoD统计量进行标签源审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算心理健康（CMH）分类器在分布偏移下性能下降，因为人类标注员和远程监督流水线奖励不同的语言信号。模型可能从特定标签源学习捷径而非真正的压力指标，导致泛化能力受限。

Method: 引入TSS（Triple-Stream Stress probe）多通道诊断框架，将文本分解为三个通道：(A)词汇字符n-grams；(B)小型形态句法通道；(C)154特征心理语言学风格通道。提出DoD（Degree of Divergence）差中之差统计量（从计量经济学改编）用于标签源审计，采用实例级自助法推断。在四个英语数据集上（N=12,906）进行测试，包括平台分层Twitter分析。

Result: 发现词汇干扰效应：在人类标注数据上向风格通道添加词汇特征会使Macro-F1下降（平均降幅0.072，p<10^-4），但在自动标注数据上无此效应。关键估计：DoD(BC-A)=0.0374，95%CI[0.0097,0.0651]，p=0.0032。Twitter平台分层分析重现该模式：DoD-Tw(BC-A)=+0.096(p<0.001)，DoD-Tw(AC-A)=-0.089(p<0.001)。干预性掩码(pos_only)在人类数据集上破坏内容词后，通道C保持95-99%的性能，表明风格通道不主要依赖词汇表层形式。

Conclusion: TSS定位为诊断审计框架而非临床筛查工具。该框架可在做出泛化性声明之前，标记出标签源特定的捷径学习问题，有助于识别和避免基于特定标注方式的模型偏差，提升计算心理健康系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Computational mental health (CMH) classifiers often degrade under distribution shift because human annotators and distant-supervision pipelines reward different linguistic signals. We introduce TSS (Triple-Stream Stress probe), a multi-channel diagnostic framework that decomposes text into (A) lexical character n-grams, (B) a small, mostly content-free morpho-syntactic channel, and (C) a 154-feature psycholinguistic style channel. Across four English datasets (N=12,906), TSS reveals a lexical interference effect: adding lexical features to the style channel reduces Macro-F1 on human-labeled data (mean drop 0.072, p<10^-4) but not on auto-labeled data. We propose Degree of Divergence (DoD), a difference-in-differences statistic adapted from econometrics for label-source auditing, with instance-level bootstrap inference; the headline estimate is DoD(BC-A) = 0.0374, 95% CI [0.0097, 0.0651], p=0.0032. A platform-stratified Twitter-only DoD (which removes the Reddit vs. Twitter contrast) reproduces the pattern with bootstrap inference: DoD-Tw(BC-A) = +0.096 (p<0.001) and DoD-Tw(AC-A) = -0.089 (p<0.001). Interventional masking (pos_only) retains ~95-99% of Channel C's performance after destroying content words on human datasets, indicating that the style channel does not rely primarily on lexical surface form. TSS is positioned as a diagnostic audit framework, not a clinical screening tool: it flags label-source-specific shortcut learning before generalization claims are made.

</details>


### [93] [ExpertIVS: Sociological Expert Driven Individual Value Simulation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20355)
*Zhen Wang,Yuqi Ren,Yuehan Cui,Hongxiang Wang,Jianxiang Peng,Zhaoxia Zhang,Bingkun Zhu,Tongxuan Zhang,Dezhi Tong,Deyi Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpertIVS框架，利用14个社会学专家智能体通过结构化专业视角解读世界价值观调查（WVS）响应，而非直接拼接响应，以提升大语言模型（LLM）智能体对个体价值系统的建模能力，并取得90.78%的价值还原保真度和显著提升的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法机械地将调查响应拼接为提示，导致语义碎片化，无法捕捉人类价值系统的内在一致性；静态选择题评估无法反映真实对话中的价值导向。因此需要一种新方法来实现更真实的社会模拟。

Method: 构建ExpertIVS框架，由14个社会学专家智能体对WVS响应进行深度语义重构，生成稳健且内在一致的个体画像；同时引入多智能体辩论机制，在动态交互中评估LLM与个体价值系统的一致性。

Result: 在来自12个国家的480个个体上实验表明，ExpertIVS达到90.78%的价值还原保真度，价值泛化性能相比基线提升5.3%，并展现出强大的人格判别力和行为一致性。

Conclusion: ExpertIVS实现了从简单响应拼接到真正社会学角色扮演的转变，显著提升了LLM智能体对个体价值系统的建模和模拟能力。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have demonstrated considerable potential for social simulation, yet struggle to accurately model individual value systems. Most existing methods mechanically stitch survey responses into prompts, which suffer from semantic fragmentation, failing to capture the internal coherence of human value systems. The value systems of LLMs are typically assessed using static multiple-choice questions, which fail to evaluate the value orientation in real-world dialogue interactions. To address these issues, we propose ExpertIVS, a framework employing 14 Sociological Expert Agents to interpret World Values Survey (WVS) responses through structured professional perspectives, rather than direct responses concatenation. These expert agents perform deep semantic reconstruction to generate robust and internally consistent individual profiles. To evaluate the consistency between LLMs and individual value systems during dynamic interactions, we further introduce a multi-agent debate mechanism. Extensive experiments across 480 individuals from 12 countries demonstrate that ExpertIVS achieves 90.78% value restoration fidelity and significantly outperforms baselines in value generalization (+5.3%). Moreover, ExpertIVS exhibits strong personality discriminability and behavioral consistency, enabling a shift from mere response concatenation to genuine sociological role-playing.

</details>


### [94] [Self-Speculation for Faster Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20359)
*Ravisri Valluri,Tung Nguyen,Aditya Grover*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SSR（Self-Speculation for Reasoning Models），一种无训练自推测解码方法，利用思维链（CoT）作为推测源，在结构化与长文本生成任务上实现最多24.1%的延迟改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在规划和多步决策任务中需要生成长推理轨迹，但这不适合语音助手或编程代理等延迟敏感型交互应用。现有加速方法主要关注token级生成，未利用推理工作流的结构性特征。

Method: SSR使用部分CoT答案分布作为草案生成器，完整CoT分布作为验证器，两者均来自同一模型在不同推理预算下的输出。利用部分CoT与完整预算响应之间的语义和词汇重叠，一次性接受长前缀草案。此外，通过后缀解码技术，利用草案种子后缀缓存，恢复超出已接受前缀的有用片段。

Result: 在多个结构化与长文本生成任务上评估SSR，对Qwen3.5和Gemma-4等开源模型实现了最高24.1%的总生成延迟相对改进。

Conclusion: SSR通过利用思维链结构进行自推测解码，为推理模型提供了一种无训练的加速方法，显著降低结构化与长文本生成任务的延迟，改善了延迟敏感应用的用户体验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are deployed for increasingly complex tasks involving planning and multi-step decision making, but high-quality performance on these tasks often requires generating long reasoning traces. This is a poor fit for latency-sensitive and interactive applications like voice assistants or coding agents, where generation latency can strongly affect user experience. Existing acceleration methods typically focus on token-level generation, without utilizing the structure of reasoning workflows. We introduce SSR: Self-Speculation for Reasoning Models, a training-free self-speculative decoding method that leverages the chain-of-thought (CoT) as a source of speculation. SSR uses the partial-CoT answer distribution as the drafter and the full-CoT distribution as the verifier, deriving both from the same model at different reasoning budgets. This builds on the observation that later partial-CoT responses often exhibit greater semantic and lexical overlap with the full-budget response. Due to this overlap, SSR can accept long draft prefixes at once, leading to large speedups on structured and long-form generation tasks. To further exploit draft-response overlap beyond the contiguous prefix accepted by standard speculative decoding, SSR also incorporates suffix decoding, using the draft to seed a suffix cache and recover useful spans beyond the accepted prefix, further reducing latency on tasks with high lexical overlap between the draft and the final response. We evaluate SSR on multiple structured and long-form generation tasks where it is most useful, and demonstrate a relative improvement of up to 24.1% on total generation latency for popular open-source models such as Qwen3.5 and Gemma-4.

</details>


### [95] [TriPLU: Bypassing the Gate with Direct Trilinear Product FFNs in Tiny Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20360)
*He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TriPLU（三次项乘积线性单元）替代传统门控FFN，在字符级TinyStories等低计算量小模型中验证其有效性；虽在特定低学习率设置下降低验证损失，但存在优化敏感性和扩展性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索轻量级decoder-only语言模型中直接采用特征投影乘法的前馈层结构是否优于现有门控机制（如SwiGLU），尤其关注固定计算预算下的小型模型性能提升潜力。

Method: 设计TriPLU架构：用三个投影流的坐标级乘法构成三次项乘积分支替代门控FFN；通过字符级TinyStories（1MB前缀）、Byte-BPE训练实验对比SwiGLU及二次/四次项对照组，采用验证损失、bits per byte(BPB)指标，并结合PMI切片分析和学习率诊断评估性能与稳定性。

Result: 在字符级TinyStories实验中，TriPLU达1.0637平均最优验证损失（SwiGLU:1.1017）；低学习率Byte-BPE实验中显著降低TinyStories/WikiText-2的BPB；PMI分析显示对中高互信息相邻token对有增益；但恒定学习率条件下高温调度仍导致最终BPB退化，且高学习率最佳检查点差距需归一化处理缓解。

Conclusion: 严格限定结论：直接乘积前馈层可在特定低计算量场景改善小模型损失，但存在强优化依赖性；未证明FLOP效率优势、可扩展性或通用大模型适用性，需进一步研究其训练动态与规模效应。

Abstract: We study whether tiny decoder-only language models benefit from feed-forward layers that directly multiply learned feature projections. TriPLU, a Trilinear Product Linear Unit, replaces the usual gated FFN branch with a product-only degree-3 branch that multiplies three projected streams coordinatewise. In a character-level TinyStories 1M-byte prefix study, TriPLU reaches a mean best validation loss of 1.0637, compared with 1.1017 for closely matched SwiGLU, 1.0780 for a degree-4 product control, and 1.1026 for a degree-2 control. In train-only Byte-BPE experiments, TriPLU also lowers validation and heldout bits per byte on TinyStories and WikiText-2 raw under low-learning-rate settings, with PMI-slice evidence suggesting gains on seen middle- and high-PMI adjacent-token pairs. Constant-learning-rate diagnostics show that product-branch normalization can reduce the high-learning-rate best-checkpoint gap, although final BPB still degrades under hot schedules. The resulting claim is deliberately narrow: direct product FFNs can improve fixed-budget small-model loss in specific low-compute regimes, but the branch is optimization-sensitive and does not establish FLOP-normalized efficiency, scaling behavior, or broad LLM performance.

</details>


### [96] [Toward Auto-Research: Mining Falsifiable Research Ideas from Paper Knowledge Graphs with Categorical Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20361)
*Yuchen Wang,Zhongzhi Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于范畴论的研究创意生成系统，将论文建模为小范畴，利用函子保留关系链来实现跨领域类比，避免大语言模型将论文简化为平面对象的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自动研究创意生成系统存在结构性弱点：将创意生成简化为自由文本重组、随机论文配对或嵌入相似度检索，均将论文视为平面对象，忽略了研究者实际使用的类型化问题-方法-指标-主张箭头关系，导致跨领域类比失准。

Method: 将每篇论文建模为小范畴C_p，对象为提取的类型化研究实体，态射为论文断言的关系；跨论文桥接为部分函子候选F: C_p → C_q，要求保持对象类型和关系类别。实现为三层算法：范畴签名聚类、函子保持门控、六轴LLM合理性评判。

Result: 在数万余篇全文解析论文上评估，在四种消融条件下，范畴门控以约17:1比例过滤跨领域候选，接受创意的定量可证伪率保持在83%以上；每个被拒候选均保留各轴理由，门控同时充当日志层。

Conclusion: 通过引入范畴论中的复合与恒等箭头，恢复论文内部及论文间结构关系，提升跨领域类比的保真度；函子保持门控有效过滤低质候选，并提供可解释的拒绝理由，为自动研究创意生成提供新的结构化工具。

Abstract: Automated research-idea generation systems built on large language models (LLMs) share a structural weakness: they reduce ideation to free-text recombination, random paper pairing, or embedding-similarity retrieval. The three approaches fail in the same way: each treats a paper as a flat object, a string or a vector, and so quotients away the typed problem-method-metric-claim arrows a researcher actually uses when reasoning about a cross-domain analogy. We recover the missing structure with the minimal piece of category theory that a typed graph alone does not provide: composition, together with identity arrows, which makes it possible to ask whether a proposed analogy preserves relation chains. Concretely, each paper $p$ is modelled as a small category $C_p$ whose objects are extracted typed research entities and whose morphisms are the relations the paper asserts; a cross-paper bridge from $p$ to $q$ is then a partial functor candidate $F: C_p -> C_q$ that preserves object kinds and covered relation classes. We instantiate the model as a three-layer algorithm: categorical signature clustering, a functor-preservation gate, and a six-axis LLM plausibility judge. Evaluated on a corpus of tens of thousands of full-text-parsed papers under four ablation conditions, the categorical gate filters cross-domain candidates at roughly a 17:1 ratio while the quantitative-falsifier rate of accepted ideas stays above 83% throughout; every rejected candidate is retained with its per-axis rationale, so the gate doubles as a logging layer rather than a silent filter.

</details>


### [97] [Multilingual Verifier Bias in RLVR: Benchmark, Rollout Diagnosis, and the Cross-Lingual Selection Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20362)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言环境下RLVR的精确匹配验证器导致显著的语言相关假阴性奖励噪声，本文提出多语言RLVR奖励审计协议并揭示跨语言选择瓶颈，发现不同语言间假阴性率差异巨大（如日语0.642 vs 英语0.122），且95%以上修复需要真正的跨语言支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR在数学推理训练中假设验证器是语言中立的，但这一假设在多语言环境中失效，导致不同语言模型训练受到不公平的奖励噪声影响，需要系统性方法来解决这一关键问题。

Method: 提出可复用审计协议包含三个组件：1）验证器鲁棒性套件；2）展开诊断程序；3）针对日、英、中文的语言条件化奖励误差指标。在MGSM和MATH-500数据集上进行评估，测试Qwen3-4B/8B和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型，使用k=8的展开设置。

Result: 发现精确匹配验证器在不同语言上拒绝正确答案的比率差异显著：Qwen3-8B在日语上假阴性率达0.642，而英语为0.122，中文为0.073。纯数字探测将机制定位到最终答案界面。跨语言聚合规则可关闭55-78%的平均选择差距，且95%以上修复需要真正的跨语言支持。规则GRPO训练提高了可信准确率但奖励误差VLB保持高位。

Conclusion: 多语言RLVR奖励在优化前必须按语言和答案界面进行系统性审计，否则将导致严重的语言偏见和训练效率低下。需要开发语言中立的验证器和跨语言训练策略以确保多语言大语言模型的公平发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a standard recipe for training large language models on mathematical reasoning, where an answer verifier serves as a language-neutral reward function. We show that this assumption fails in multilingual settings: an exact-match verifier turns format and script variation into language-dependent false-negative reward noise. We introduce a reusable protocol for auditing multilingual RLVR rewards: a verifier-robustness suite, a rollout-diagnosis procedure, and language-conditioned reward-error metrics for Japanese, English, and Chinese answers. On MGSM rollouts with k=8, the exact-match proxy rejects trusted-correct answers at sharply different rates by language across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct; for Qwen3-8B, the false-negative rate reaches 0.642 on JP against 0.122 on EN and 0.073 on CN. A plain-numeric probe localizes the mechanism to the final-answer interface: an interface model drives reward-error VLB to zero while the residual accuracy gap is unchanged. We then expose a cross-lingual selection bottleneck: on MGSM250 rollouts, a target-local aggregation rule using no trusted labels closes 55-78% of the average selection gap, and over 95% of repairs require genuine cross-lingual support. The bottleneck replicates on a 483-problem MATH-500 set. A controlled training audit shows that rule-GRPO raises trusted accuracy while the reward-error VLB stays high. The unifying message is operational: multilingual RLVR rewards should be audited by language and by answer interface before they are optimized.

</details>


### [98] [Hadith computational science in the age of large language models: a critical narrative review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20364)
*Md. Ashraful Haque,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判性地评估了transformer模型、检索增强管道和大语言模型(LLMs)如何重塑圣训计算科学。研究发现进展不均衡：数据资源扩展、分割任务成熟化、叙述者和来源验证问题形式化程度提高，LLM辅助工作流支持语料库规模增强和多语言访问；但同时存在语料库狭窄、基准可比性弱、合成到真实迁移差距、叙述者身份解析、预处理脆弱性、可复现性有限和专家验证稀疏等限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述文献记录了圣训计算领域文献的增长，但缺乏对哪些进展具有方法论稳健性、哪些仍然受基准限制、以及哪些未解决问题仍然限制学术使用的批判性评估。

Method: 采用批判性叙述综述方法，结合对现有综述的批判、对代表性原创研究的论文级评估，以及伊斯兰学者和领域专家关于真实性、权威性和负责任使用的视角综合。

Result: 发现进展不均衡：(1)积极进展：数据资源扩展、分割任务成熟、叙述者和来源验证问题形式化改善、LLM辅助工作流支持语料库规模增强、多语言访问和有根据的评估；(2)持续限制：语料库狭窄、基准可比性弱、合成到真实迁移差距、叙述者身份解析困难、预处理脆弱性、可复现性有限、专家验证稀疏；(3)重要差距：非经典和生僻语料、注释和解释性文献、与古兰经和先知传说的跨来源联系、以及法基赫(Fiqh)面临的证据支持。

Conclusion: 圣训计算应被评估为证据基础设施问题而非孤立的模型性能，需要知识整合、溯源和专家监督。基于此，本文提出了使该领域方法论更强大且对伊斯兰学术研究更有用的研究议程。

Abstract: We examine how hadith computational science is being reshaped by transformer models, retrieval-grounded pipelines, and large language models (LLMs). Recent reviews document growth in the literature, but they do not yet provide a critical account of which advances are methodologically robust, which remain benchmark-bound, and which unresolved problems still limit scholarly use. We address this gap through a critical narrative review that combines critique of existing reviews, paper-level appraisal of representative original studies, and synthesis of Islamic scholar and domain-expert perspectives on authenticity, authority, and responsible use. We find uneven progress. Data resources have expanded, segmentation tasks have matured, narrator and source-verification problems are better formalized, and LLM-assisted workflows now support corpus-scale enrichment, multilingual access, and grounded evaluation. At the same time, progress remains constrained by narrow corpora, weak benchmark comparability, synthetic-to-real transfer gaps, narrator identity resolution, preprocessing fragility, limited reproducibility, and sparse expert-grounded validation. We show that important gaps lie beyond dominant benchmarks: non-canonical and obscure corpora, commentary and explanatory literature, cross-source links with Qur'an and seerah, and fiqh-facing evidence support. We argue that hadith computation should be assessed less as isolated model performance than as an evidence infrastructure problem requiring knowledge integration, provenance, and expert supervision. On this basis, we define a research agenda for making the field methodologically stronger and more useful to Islamic scholarship.

</details>


### [99] [Trilingual Topic Modeling of Sri Lankan Parliamentary Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20365)
*Himath Dhanapala,Haren Daishika,Himandhi Kuruppu,Sithija Seneviratne,Ashini Kavindya,Patalee Narasinghe,Sandeepa Weerasekara,Nisansa de Silva,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端框架，利用大语言模型提取多语言混合语料文本，通过多语言嵌入与密度聚类进行主题建模，并引入BiTopic扩展提升可解释性。应用于斯里兰卡议会辩论语料，成功提取30个宏观主题，聚类纯度0.673，主题时间线与国家重大事件吻合，克服了传统LDA的跨语言碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡议会辩论语料为三语（僧伽罗语、泰米尔语、英语）混合编码文本，因PDF版式复杂、多语言书写系统及黏着语形态而难以被现有NLP流程处理。传统LDA因跨语言碎片化失败。因此需要一种能处理多语言混合语料的端到端主题建模方法。

Method: 采用基于大语言模型的文本提取模块，结合多语言嵌入表示与基于密度的聚类算法进行主题建模。进一步探索混合语义-词法扩展BiTopic以增强可解释性并减少噪声丢弃。将该框架应用于2017-2026年共19,553篇议会演讲语料。

Result: 模型提取出30个宏观主题，聚类纯度(BCP)达0.673。主题的时间演变趋势与2019年复活节星期日袭击事件及2022年经济危机等重大国家事件无监督对齐。传统LDA在该语料上失败，而本文方法成功在三种语言上识别出主题结构。

Conclusion: 所提端到端框架能够无监督地处理复杂多语言混合议会语料，有效克服PDF解析、多语言脚本及形态学挑战，实现跨语言主题识别与解释，并可与真实世界事件关联。

Abstract: Sri Lankan parliamentary debates (Hansards) constitute a trilingual corpus of speeches in Sinhala, Tamil, and English, including code-mixed content, yet remain inaccessible to standard NLP pipelines due to layout-complex PDFs, multilingual scripts, and agglutinative morphology. We present an end-to-end framework that addresses these challenges through LLM-based text extraction followed by a multilingual embedding and density-based clustering pipeline for topic modeling. A hybrid semantic-lexical extension, BiTopic, is further explored to improve interpretability and recover speeches otherwise discarded as noise. Applied to 19,553 speeches spanning 2017-2026, the pipeline recovers 30 macro-topics achieving a cluster purity (BCP) of 0.673, whose temporal trajectories align unsupervised with major national events including the 2019 Easter Sunday attacks and the 2022 economic crisis. Traditional LDA fails on this corpus due to cross-lingual fragmentation, whereas the proposed approach successfully identifies thematic structure across all three languages without supervision.

</details>


### [100] [Research Paper Quality Recognition Through Textual Feature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20368)
*Saikiran Korla,Sadwik Gummadavelli,Trung-Nghia Le,Minh-Triet Tran,Tam V. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个仅基于论文标题和摘要文本特征的研究论文质量分类基准，将论文分为"良好"（高被引）和"非良好"（被撤稿）两类。实验表明，FastText结合SVM可达到91.12%的准确率，SBERT结合神经网络达到87.22%的准确率，证明了文本信息在评估研究质量方面的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究的知识和创新依赖于其质量和可信度，但区分有影响力的高质量研究与有缺陷的研究仍然是一个挑战。本文旨在开发一个学术诚信工具，促进可信赖的学术研究，为研究质量评估提供自动化的基准方法。

Method: 本文构建了一个二分类任务，将研究论文分为"良好"（高被引）和"非良好"（被撤稿）两类。仅使用论文标题和摘要的文本特征作为输入。评估了多种嵌入技术（SBERT、Word2Vec、FastText、USE、TF-IDF）与多种分类器（支持向量机、随机森林、神经网络）的组合。贡献包括：(1)超参数透明度，(2)使用t-SNE进行特征空间可视化，(3)使用SHAP进行模型可解释性分析，(4)详细分析错误案例。

Result: 实验结果表明，FastText嵌入结合SVM分类器达到了最高的91.12%准确率，而SBERT嵌入结合神经网络模型达到了87.22%的准确率。这些发现表明，仅基于标题和摘要的文本信息就能有效区分高质量和低质量的研究论文，证明了文本特征在评估研究质量方面的潜在价值。

Conclusion: 本文证明仅基于论文标题和摘要的文本信息就能有效评估研究质量，FastText+SVM组合表现最佳。这项工作为开发促进学术诚信和可信赖研究的工具做出了贡献，同时提出了部署此类系统的伦理考量，为学术出版和研究质量监控提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge and innovations are shaped by using the quality and credibility of the scientific research. Yet, distinguishing between impactful, high-quality work and flawed studies remains a challenge. This paper introduces a benchmark for classifying research papers into two categories: good (highly cited) and non-good (retracted), using only textual features from titles and abstracts. We evaluate multiple embedding techniques, including SBERT, Word2Vec, FastText, USE, and TF-IDF, combined with classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and Neural Networks. Our contributions include: (1) hyperparameter transparency, (2) feature space visualizations using t-SNE, (3) model interpretability analysis with SHAP, and (4) detailed examination of error cases. Experimental results show that a neural network with SBERT embeddings achieves 87.22\% accuracy, while FastText combined with SVM reaches 91.12\%. These findings highlight the value of textual information in assessing research quality, with ethical considerations for deployment. This work contributes toward the development of academic integrity tools that promote trustworthy scholarship.

</details>


### [101] [ASTAR: Automated induction of STAndardized radiology Reporting templates from large-scale clinical free-text corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20369)
*Xinfeng Zhang,Mingxuan Liu,Yifei Chen,Juncheng Zhu,Kasidit Anmahapong,Yiming Huang,Yuan Zhang,Hongjia Yang,Yi Liao,Gang Ning,Haibo Qu,Qiyuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Structured reporting converts free-text radiology narratives into queryable data keys, facilitating cohort assembly, longitudinal tracking, and training label generation for medical AI. The prevailing paradigm follows a two-stage pipeline: (1) constructing a reporting template, (2) extracting information to populate it. While the extraction stage has benefited from advances in large language models (LLMs), template construction remains a manual bottleneck relying on labor-intensive expert consensus that is static, difficult to scale, and may fail to capture real-world reporting diversity. We address this limitation with \textbf{\texttt{ASTAR}}, an LLM-based framework for Automated induction of STAndardized radiology Reporting templates from large-scale clinical free-text corpora. Extensive experiments on 4,215 fetal brain MRI reports from multiple centers demonstrate that the \textbf{\texttt{ASTAR}}-induced template surpasses two expert-curated templates across template coverage, information fidelity, diagnostic fidelity, and expert-rated usability, reducing template development from weeks of committee deliberation to hours of automated processing. Code: https://github.com/birthlab/ASTAR

</details>


### [102] [When Do LLMs Replace Fine-Tuned NLU? A Decision Framework for Intent Detection in Production Conversational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20371)
*Carson Rodrigues,Oysturn Vas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 零样本大语言模型能否替代微调NLU分类器进行意图识别？本研究通过严格对比实验发现：结论取决于意图空间特性。在标签充足场景下，微调模型（如RoBERTa）性能更优且成本/速度优势显著；但在跨域检测、抗噪声及动态意图体系等生产场景中，LLM展现独特价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对“零样本LLM可取代微调NLU分类器”的流行观点，本研究旨在通过头对头实验验证该主张，解决工业界在模型选型中的关键决策困惑。

Method: 在完整ATIS和CLINC150数据集上，对比四种方法：微调RoBERTa、TF-IDF+逻辑回归基线、句子嵌入kNN及Claude Haiku零样本模型。采用自举95%置信区间与配对显著性检验，并重点测试三个生产场景：越界检测(OOS)、ASR噪声鲁棒性（通过TTS→Whisper噪声管道）及动态意图体系适配能力。

Result: ① ATIS数据集：RoBERTa(95.9)显著优于Claude零样本(84.1, Δ=11.8, p<0.001)，且计算成本降低3个数量级；② CLINC150广域意图：RoBERTa(89.1)与Claude(88.5)统计无差异(p=0.24)；③ LLM优势场景：OOS召回率(85.6 vs RoBERTa 58.1)、0dB噪声下准确率(92.5 vs 80.0)、跨应用意图迁移（新应用零微调下维持~94%准确率 vs 微调模型0%）。

Conclusion: 提出实践决策框架：标签充足且意图固定时优选微调模型；需快速部署新意图体系、强抗噪或高精度OOS检测时应选LLM。LLM无法全面替代微调模型，但特定生产场景具有不可替代价值。

Abstract: A common claim is that zero-shot large language models (LLMs) can replace fine-tuned NLU classifiers for intent detection. We test this claim head-to-head and find that the honest answer is: it depends on the intent space. On full ATIS and CLINC150 we compare a fine-tuned RoBERTa, a TF-IDF+logistic-regression baseline, sentence-embedding kNN, and Claude Haiku zero-shot, reporting bootstrap 95% confidence intervals and paired significance tests. When abundant in-domain labels exist, fine-tuned RoBERTa is as good or better and three orders of magnitude cheaper and faster: on ATIS it beats Claude zero-shot by 11.8 points (95.9 vs. 84.1, p<0.001). On the broad 150-intent CLINC150 schema the two are statistically tied (89.1 vs. 88.5, p=0.24): the LLM matches a fully supervised model with no training data. The LLM's advantages appear in three production-relevant regimes: out-of-scope detection (OOS recall 85.6 vs. 58.1 for RoBERTa); robustness to realistic ASR noise via a controlled text-to-speech to noise to Whisper pipeline (92.5 vs. 80.0 at 0 dB); and dynamic per-deployment schemas, where a classifier trained on one app's intents scores 0% on a new app's intents while the schema-prompted LLM serves both at ~94% with zero retraining. We distill these findings into a decision framework for practitioners.

</details>


### [103] [An ambiguity taxonomy for evaluating large language model performance on clinical registry abstraction: a multi-site prospective study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20373)
*James Matheson,Betsy Castillo,Andrew Y. Shin,David Scheinker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型(LLM)在未处理的电子医疗记录(EMR)数据上进行临床注册摘要任务的性能评估。LLM准确率为87-91.5%，但显著低于人工摘要员的98%一致性；随着问题模糊性和临床推理要求增加，LLM准确性从96%(药物/事件标记)降至62%(事件时间)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床注册数据摘要需要大量人工标注工作，研究旨在评估LLM能否在未处理的EMR数据上自动完成此任务，减少人工工作量并提高效率。

Method: 采用两阶段研究设计：(1)试点研究：LLM识别各注册问题的候选数据源，人工摘要员据此定义问题特定文档集；(2)验证研究：LLM使用问题特定文档集回答问题。两名摘要员独立建立真实标准并回答问题分类(6类，按模糊性和临床推理要求排序)。

Result: 分析样本包含9,430个摘要员答案(合并为4,715个共识答案)。人工评分者间一致性约98%，LLM答案87%与共识完全匹配、2%部分匹配、9%不匹配。157个至少20个答案的问题的平均准确率91.5%(SD 13.4%)，随模糊性增加而下降。

Conclusion: LLM在未处理EMR数据上的临床注册问题回答准确性显著低于人工摘要员。LLM准确性随问题模糊性和所需临床推理水平增加而稳步下降，表明在当前技术下LLM尚不能完全替代人工进行复杂临床数据摘要工作。

Abstract: Objective: To evaluate large language model (LLM) performance on unprocessed electronic medical record (EMR) data for clinical registry abstraction. Methods: We evaluated LLM performance answering registry questions for the American College of Cardiology National Cardiovascular Data Registry (ACC NCDR). In a pilot study at an academic medical center, the model identified candidate data sources for each registry question and experienced abstractors used these results to define question-specific document sets. In a validation study at a second center with a second ACC NCDR registry, the LLM answered questions using the question-specific document sets. Before reviewing any output, two abstractors independently established the ground truth and assigned each question to one of six categories, ordered by the ambiguity and clinical reasoning required to resolve it: Medication/Event Flag, Binary Clinical Presence, Administrative, Quantitative Laboratory/Physiologic, Clinical Interpretation, and Event Timing. Results: The analytical sample comprised 9,430 abstractor answers reconciled to 4,715 consensus answers (501 pilot; 4,214 validation). In the pilot, candidate data sources per question averaged between 14.6 (SD 13.9) for demographics and 89.2 (SD 56.1) for history and risk factors. In validation, human inter-rater agreement was approximately 98\% while 87\% of LLM answers exactly matched consensus, 2\% partially, and 9\% did not. Mean question-level accuracy was 91.5\% (SD 13.4\%) across 157 questions with at least 20 answers, and declined as ambiguity increased, from 96\% for Medication/Event Flag to 62\% for Event Timing questions. Conclusions: LLMs answering clinical registry questions on unprocessed EMR data achieved far lower accuracy than human abstractors. LLM accuracy fell steadily as ambiguity and the level of required clinical reasoning increased.

</details>


### [104] [VA-DPO: Valence-Arousal Direct Preference Optimization for Controllable Emotion Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20374)
*Hyunwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VA-DPO方法，通过连续效价-唤醒平面精确控制语言模型的情感生成，显著降低目标偏差并提升情感相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感生成研究多依赖离散情感标签（如快乐、愤怒），无法表达细微情感（如“轻微沮丧但平静”），亟需连续情感控制机制以实现更精准的生成。

Method: VA-DPO方法对直接偏好优化（DPO）进行改进：使用冻结的效价-唤醒（VA）回归器计算生成文本到目标点的欧几里得距离，仅保留距离差距超过阈值τ的候选样本对，并以标准DPO损失优化LoRA适配器，参考模型保持冻结。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，平均VA距离较系统提示减少33%、较少样本提示减少25%，效价/唤醒相关性提升至r_v=0.93和r_a=0.75；效果迁移至Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-3B，且无负面影响（MMLU不变，HellaSwag和TruthfulQA性能保留）。

Conclusion: VA-DPO方法有效实现连续情感控制，开源代码和偏好构造管道增强了可复现性，为情感计算领域提供了高效、鲁棒的解决方案。
