We evaluate these methods across six language pairs varying in typological distance, resourcedness, and document length. Averaged over three models, CTFAlign reduces word alignment error rate from 0.412 to 0.326. These gains transfer downstream, leading to improvements in document-level translation coverage evaluation and recognition of semantic differences. We release CTFAlign as a Python package and make the code and data to reproduce our experiments publicly available.

</details>


### [141] [Evidence-Consistent Generative Detection under Scenario-Level Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21043)
*San Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Conventional in-distribution evaluation can overestimate robustness when training and test data share recurring task-specific patterns or surface cues. This risk is especially relevant in social-engineering fraud detection, where attackers can preserve malicious intent while changing the scenario, impersonated entity, or wording. We study this problem as scenario-level out-of-distribution (SL-OOD) detection for SMS and voice phishing, where entire attack scenarios are held out from training while the label space remains fixed. This setting tests whether models can generalize to unseen attack scenarios using decision-relevant evidence rather than familiar scenario-specific cues. Using this SL-OOD evaluation, we find that high in-distribution performance does not reliably predict held-out robustness across feature-, encoder-, and decoder-based baselines. We interpret this gap as scenario memorization: reliance on recurring scenario-specific lexical or entity cues rather than decision-relevant evidence. We propose ECoG, an evidence-consistent generative framework that combines evidence-span supervision with a rationale-label consistency objective during training. On the 0.5B decoder, relative to the same backbone trained without consistency regularization, ECoG raises Macro-F1 on OOD challenging instances by 3.22 points, reduces the share of predictions whose generated rationale supports the opposite label by 4.22 points, and increases token-level overlap with reference evidence spans by 8.38 points; the reduction in prediction-rationale inconsistency is consistent across four decoder backbones. These results suggest that compact generative detectors can benefit from evidence supervision and rationale-label consistency under social-engineering shift.

</details>


### [142] [PromptResponse: Optimizing Prompts for LLM Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21074)
*Erik Thureck,Robert Kühnen,Tim Jacobowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验研究发现，提示词格式（特别是JSON格式）能显著提升LLM生成代码的效率和语法稳定性，而LLM微调提示词则显著降低任务性能。低成本的格式重排即可产生可测量的改进，但微调需考虑模型对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在研究工作流程和软件开发流程中的使用日益增加，但其输出对输入提示词的变化仍然敏感，亟需研究提示词格式和调优对生成代码质量的影响。

Method: 提出PromptResponse受控研究，使用HumanEval数据集的5个语义相同但句法不同的变体（基线、JSON、Markdown、YAML和LLM微调版本），在8200次执行中使用GPT-4o解决编码问题，评估代码的性能、效率和稳定性。

Result: 一致的格式（特别是JSON）提高了生成效率和语法稳定性，任务性能有轻微提升；而LLM微调提示词导致任务性能显著下降，其他维度无显著改善。

Conclusion: 低成本的格式重排即可产生可测量的改进，而微调必须考虑模型对齐。研究提供了基于结果的实用建议，并发布了数据集变体和评估管道供未来研究使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in research workflows and software development pipelines, yet their output remains sensitive to input prompt variations. This paper presents $\unicode{x00AB}$PromptResponse$\unicode{x00BB}$, a controlled study examining how formatting and LLM-based tuning of coding task prompts affect the resulting code's performance, efficiency, and stability. Using five semantically identical yet syntactically distinct variants of the HumanEval dataset$\unicode{x2014}$baseline, JSON, Markdown, YAML, and an LLM-tuned version$\unicode{x2014}$we had GPT-4o solve its coding problems over 8200$\unicode{x00A0}$executions. Our results show that consistent formatting$\unicode{x2014}$especially JSON$\unicode{x2014}$improves generation efficiency and syntactic stability, with minor gains in task performance. Conversely, the LLM-tuned prompts resulted in significantly degraded task performance without significant improvements in any other dimension. These findings suggest that low-effort reformatting alone can yield measurable improvements, while tuning must account for model alignment. We conclude our work with providing a set of practical recommendations informed by our results as well as releasing our dataset variants and evaluation pipeline for future work.

</details>


### [143] [Jokes Aside: Measuring the Semantic Distance of Double Meanings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21087)
*Fabio De Ponte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用大语言模型的上下文嵌入向量重新评估经典笑话生成指标，发现"对称性"指标与高评分笑话显著相关，但现有指标整体预测能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算幽默研究中，大语言模型为自动生成笑话提供了新工具。研究旨在利用新兴的上下文嵌入技术验证并改进Petrovic等人提出的经典笑话生成假设及其衍生指标。

Method: 1. 使用text-embedding-3-small和MiniLM模型提取词嵌入向量；2. 基于三个数据集(JokeJudger/Expunations/rJokes)重新评估obviousness、compatibility、comparison三个指标；3. 创新性地提出"对称性"(symmetry)指标衡量Z与X/Y的接近程度；4. 扩展数据集以包含玩笑核心歧义表达的两种含义配对句。

Result: 1. 所有模型在JokeJudger上最高准确率仅57.1%(低于61.5%基准)，在Expunations和rJokes表现更差；2. 对称性指标在所有测试中 consistently 与高评分笑话关联；3. 现有指标整体预测幽默评分能力较弱。

Conclusion: 尽管基于嵌入向量的指标体系未能有效提升幽默预测能力，但新提出的"对称性"指标揭示了其作为幽默必要但不充分条件的特性，为后续研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models have significantly enriched the toolkit for computational humor research, particularly in the automated generation of jokes and puns. A key innovation, contextual embedding vectors, offers new opportunities to revisit and refine earlier hypotheses. Notably, Petrovic and Matthews (2013) proposed a joke generation model based on the scheme "I like my X like I like my Y, Z" (e.g. "I like my ice like I like my dreams, crushed"). They suggested that joke hilarity increases with: a) frequent association of Z with X and Y, b) rarity of Z, c) ambiguity of Z, and d) meaning distance between X and Y. Building on this, Winters et al. (2019) proposed a set of metrics, based on Google Ngrams and Word2Vector. In this work, three out of their five metrics are revisited with word embeddings: obviousness, compatibility, and comparison. Another measure, symmetry, defined as closeness of Z to both X and Y, is introduced here for the first time. Two models were used to collect the embedding vectors (OpenAI text-embedding-3-small and MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2) on three datasets: JokeJudger, Expunations, and rJokes. The last two datasets, Expunations, and rJokes, were expanded by adding paired sentences that captured the ambiguous expression at the core of each joke in its two different meanings. Results revealed that models trained on the proposed metrics performed poorly in predicting humor ratings: on JokeJudger, the best model achieved 57.1% accuracy, below the 61.5% baseline, while performance on Expunations and rJokes was even lower. Nevertheless, the symmetry metric seems consistently associated with higher-rated jokes, suggesting it may capture a necessary -though not sufficient- property of humor.

</details>


### [144] [When the Feature Pool Goes Algorithmic: Extending Mufwene's Ecology of Language Evolution to LLM-Mediated Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21088)
*Kunmei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为大型语言模型应被视为"分布性中介"，通过算法重新加权影响语言变体到达人类选择者的相对频率，但人类社会评价仍然是决定性的，模型输出可能扩散、被视为"AI风格"而避免，或根本无法扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mufwene的生态模型将语言演化定位于个体惯用语变体之间的竞争以及说话者对互动中获得的语言材料的选择，而大型语言模型（LLMs）使这一架构复杂化。作者旨在探讨LLMs如何影响语言演化过程，以及选择机制是否仍然保留在人类说话者身上。

Method: 理论论证与概念框架构建。将LLMs定义为"分布性中介"，提出"算法重新加权"理论框架，即模型通过训练和后处理聚合、转换并重分配人类语言数据，从而影响语言变体到达人类选择者的频率分布。通过分析模型特定语言特征和词汇采用证据，检验该理论路径的合理性。

Result: 现有证据表明模型特定的语言特征和词汇采用与该理论路径的部分内容一致，但并未确立必然收敛性。LLMs可以改变竞争变体的相对频率，但人类社会评价仍然起决定性作用：模型相关形式可能扩散并惯例化、被社会识别为"AI风格"随后被避免，或根本不能扩散。

Conclusion: 本文提出的理论框架将Mufwene的特征池生态学向说话者选择上游延伸一步，认为LLMs作为分布性中介改变了语言生态，但并未将选择机制从人类说话者身上转移。该框架产生关于采用、模型版本效应、收敛性和社会逆转的可检验预测，强调了算法中介与人类语言选择之间的复杂互动关系。

Abstract: Mufwene's ecological model locates language evolution in competition among variants contributed by individual idiolects and in speakers' selection from linguistic material made available through interaction. Large language models (LLMs) complicate this architecture without requiring the locus of selection to move away from human speakers. This article argues that LLMs are best treated as distributional mediators: they aggregate language produced across human populations, transform its distribution through training and post-training, and redistribute model-specific outputs at scale. I call the resulting ecological process algorithmic reweighting of the speaker-accessible distribution: model mediation can alter the relative frequencies with which competing variants reach human selectors. Emerging evidence on model-specific linguistic profiles and lexical uptake is consistent with parts of this pathway, but does not establish inevitable convergence. Human social evaluation remains decisive: model-associated forms may diffuse and become conventionalized, become socially recognizable as 'AI-like' and subsequently avoided, or fail to diffuse in the first place. The proposal extends Mufwene's feature-pool ecology one step upstream of speaker selection and yields testable predictions about uptake, model-version effects, convergence, and social reversal.

</details>


### [145] [No PUN Intended: Plausible Unknown Names for Person-Centred LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21206)
*Dimitri Staufer,David Hartmann,Ibrahim Baroud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM评估中使用人名带来的混淆问题，提出PUN协议，构建并验证“可信未知人名”，即形式合理但无索引证据的人名。发布了300个此类人名，实验表明这些人名比对照组更似真实人名，参与者仅3%能找回相关证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人名在LLM事实性、隐私泄露、偏见和放弃评估中广泛用作提示变量，但当人名的证据状态不受控制时，测量可能混淆记忆、检索、姓名先验和错误归因。因此需要一种方法来构建和验证“未知人名”，以控制评估的混淆因素。

Method: 提出PUN（可信未知人名）协议，结合Wikidata衍生组件、Web启用的LLM筛选和受控搜索重新验证来构建和验证未知人名。通过接受率、可重现性、消融实验和204名参与者的人机研究来评估协议效果。

Result: 接受的人名比对照组更似真实人名；参与者在仅3%的情况下能恢复人员证据。发布了300个与对照组的比较人名。

Conclusion: PUN协议能有效构建可信未知人名，解决LLM评估中因人名证据状态不受控导致的混淆问题，为后续研究提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Person names are widely used as prompt variables in LLM evaluations of factuality, privacy leakage, bias and abstention, but when a name's evidential status is uncontrolled, measurements may conflate memorisation, retrieval, name priors and wrong-person attribution. We operationalise an unknown name as one with plausible First-Last form, no indexed full-name evidence, and no ambiguity signals under a documented validation run, and introduce PUN (Plausible Unknown Names), a protocol for constructing and validating such names, combining Wikidata-derived components, web-enabled LLM screening, and controlled search revalidation. We report acceptance rate, reproducibility, ablations, and a 204-participant human study, finding accepted names are more name-like than controls while participants recover person evidence in only 3% of cases. We release 300 names with comparison controls.

</details>


### [146] [RARE: Decoupling Representation Steering from Expert Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21236)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对表示工程在Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型上直接应用时产生的结构失配问题，提出了一种无路由器感知框架RARE。该方法将行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间以消除路由器可见分量，并校正传播到下游层的路由漂移。在有害性、真实性和事实编辑三个场景下的评估表明，RARE在保持MMLU准确率的同时显著提升了攻击成功率、TruthfulQA准确率和CounterFact效能，证明路由一致性是MoE模型适应表示工程的重要架构考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示工程通过修改中间隐藏状态为控制语言模型行为提供了轻量级手段，但其直接应用于Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型时会产生结构失配。经验研究证实了这一失败模式，发现保持清洁的路由可显著恢复导向性能，且路由对语义内容的敏感度高于受控内容下的行为变化。这一发现激发了开发适用于MoE语言模型的无路由器感知表示工程框架的需求。

Method: 作者提出了RARE（无路由器感知表示工程框架），该框架将任意行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间上，从而移除路由器可见分量，并进一步校正传播到所选下游层的路由漂移。为确定此框架中最佳扰动估计器，他们在六个异构开源权重MoE模型上评估了五种估计器，涵盖有害性导向、真实性导向和事实编辑三个场景。

Result: 在有害性导向方面，RARE的平均攻击成功率达到53.3%，同时保持67.8%的MMLU准确率，产生了比基线更强的综合效能-效用权衡。此外，它将平均TruthfulQA MC1准确率从41.0%提升至58.6%，将CounterFact效能从16.8%提升至96.3%。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将路由一致性作为将表示工程适应于MoE模型的重要架构考量。RARE框架通过消除路由器可见分量和校正路由漂移，成功解决了MoE模型与表示工程之间的结构失配问题，在多个任务上取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Representation engineering offers a lightweight means of controlling language-model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states, but its direct application to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models introduces a structural mismatch. We first verify this failure mode through a series of empirical studies and find that preserving clean routing substantially recovers steering performance and that routing is more sensitive to semantic content than to behavioral changes under controlled content. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RARE, a router-agnostic representation engineering framework for MoE language models. RARE projects arbitrary behavioral perturbations onto the null space of the router matrix, thereby removing router-visible components, and further corrects routing drift propagated to selected downstream layers. To decide the best perturbation estimator in this framework, we evaluate five estimators on six heterogeneous open-weight MoE models across three steering scenarios: harmfulness, truthfulness, and factual editing. On harmfulness steering, RARE reaches an average attack success rate of 53.3% while retaining 67.8% MMLU accuracy, yielding a stronger aggregate effectiveness--utility trade-off than baselines. It further improves average TruthfulQA MC1 accuracy from 41.0% to 58.6% and CounterFact efficacy from 16.8% to 96.3%. These results support routing consistency as an important architectural consideration for adapting representation engineering to MoE models.

</details>


### [147] [Affective Context Amplifies Sycophancy in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21242)
*Jiayi Li,Sanjana Menon,Brett Frischmann,Shomir Wilson,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大型语言模型在用户情感状态下表现出的谄媚行为，发现模型在面对用户负面情感（如孤独、痛苦）时会软化或回避批评性反馈，可能阻碍用户获得必要的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究大型语言模型作为对话伙伴时，其对用户情感状态的感知是否会影响其在主观评价性交互中的谄媚倾向，以及这种倾向是否会导致在用户最需要时抑制关键反馈。

Method: 研究方法基于谄媚理论，将谄媚定义为模型独立评价与用户面向响应之间的差异。通过向七个大型语言模型展示同一内容（以第三方叙述或用户自述形式），并分析两个Reddit数据集（r/AmItheAsshole和r/TrueUnpopularOpinion），测量这种差异。

Result: 结果表明，这种差异是系统性的且单向的，用户面向响应往往软化或保留负面或反对性判断。情感背景进一步放大了这种差异，尤其是负面情绪状态（如孤独和痛苦）效应最大，模型倾向于采取回避性谄媚，即退缩为非承诺性响应而非直接同意。

Conclusion: 结论指出，情感背景作为脆弱性信号，在用户最需要时抑制了关键反馈，通常通过回避性谄媚实现，这表明模型可能因迎合用户情感而未能提供必要的诚实反馈，存在潜在风险。

Abstract: As conversational companions, large language models (LLMs) often have access to users' emotional states. We study how this affective context modulates LLM sycophancy in subjective, evaluative interactions, where users share actions or opinions that invite feedback. Drawing on ingratiation theory, we measure sycophancy as the divergence between a model's independent evaluation and its user-facing response, elicited by presenting the same content as either a third-party account or the user's own disclosure. Across seven LLMs and two Reddit datasets (r/AmItheAsshole and r/TrueUnpopularOpinion), we find that this divergence is systematic and strongly one-directional. User-facing responses consistently soften or withhold negative or oppositional judgments. Affective context further amplifies this divergence with negative states, particularly loneliness and distress, producing the largest effects. These findings suggest that affective context functions as a vulnerability signal that suppresses critical feedback when users may need it most, often through evasive sycophancy, in which models retreat toward non-committal responses rather than outright agreement.

</details>


### [148] [Benchmarking Patent Drafting from Inventor-Style Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21249)
*Lekang Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Stephan Goetz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Dis2Pat数据集与Patent-MAF框架，解决现有大语言模型无法直接从非正式发明披露生成完整专利申请的核心痛点，验证了当前LLM在真实专利撰写场景中的局限性，并提供可本地部署的强基线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度依赖后期结构化输入，忽视真实专利流程始于发明人撰写的非法律化早期披露材料这一关键挑战。

Method: 构建反映真实工作流的披露到专利数据集Dis2Pat，并设计支持隐私保护的多智能体框架Patent-MAF实现本地化专利生成。

Result: 基准测试显示当前LLM存在显著局限，Patent-MAF持续优于开源模型，性能接近大型闭源模型。

Conclusion: 验证了从非结构化发明披露到完整专利申请的技术可行性，提出兼顾实用性与隐私需求的解决方案，为领域发展建立新标杆。

Abstract: While recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising results on individual patent drafting tasks, they fundamentally fail to investigate the core challenge of real-world patent drafting: generating a complete and legally coherent patent application directly from early-stage invention materials. Prior work predominantly assumes later-stage, highly structured, or already legalistic inputs. However, real patenting workflows begin with informal, de-legalized disclosures authored by inventors. To bridge the gap, we introduce Dis2Pat, a disclosure-to-patent dataset that reflects realistic patenting workflows by requiring the generation of complete patent applications directly from inventor-style, de-legalized disclosures. Given the inherent difficulty of long-form, legally constrained patent drafting and the strong privacy requirements, we further propose a strong baseline named Patent-MAF. It is a multi-agent framework for locally deployable patent drafting. Benchmark results reveal that current LLMs exhibit limitations in patent drafting, while Patent-MAF provides a strong baseline that consistently outperforms evaluated open-source models and remains competitive with large closed-source models.

</details>


### [149] [EnSI-RAG: Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21252)
*Xuanyu Meng,Jiashuo Sun,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EnSI-RAG框架，通过构建实体中心索引解决长文档问答中证据碎片化和多跳推理的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强生成（RAG）方法通常将文档索引为原始块并通过嵌入相似度检索，这在实体与支撑证据被块边界分离或问题需要跨语料库的多跳推理时，性能会显著下降。

Method: 提出EnSI-RAG框架，构建查询无关的实体中心索引。每条记录包含实体(e)、类型(t)、语义类别(k，如属性、关系、方面)及数值(v)，并保留原文链接。检索时利用这些记录作为句柄，由大语言模型（LLM）综合检索到的段落生成最终答案，实现了证据定位与答案合成的分离。

Result: 在Loong和Oolong基准测试中，EnSI-RAG的平均准确率达到78.24%，相较于参考的基线分数提升了6.62分。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了该框架在复杂长文档问答场景下的有效性，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over long, connected documents remains challenging because relevant evidence may span multiple entities and their relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods typically index documents as raw chunks and retrieve them through embedding similarity. Their performance degrades when chunk boundaries separate entities from supporting evidence or when a question requires multi-hop reasoning across the corpus. We propose EnSI-RAG (Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a framework that constructs a query-independent, entity-centered index. Each record (e, t, k, v) represents an entity e, its type t, a semantic category k in {property, relation, aspect}, and a value v, while retaining links to the original source passages. At query time, these records serve as retrieval handles, and an LLM synthesizes the retrieved passages into the final answer. This design separates evidence localization from answer synthesis while preserving traceable source evidence. Across Loong and Oolong, EnSI-RAG achieves an average accuracy of 78.24. Relative to the published baseline scores used as references, this is 6.62 points higher, suggesting its effectiveness across these settings. The code is available at https://github.com/RamonMeng/EnSI-RAG.

</details>


### [150] [Memory Augmentation Unlocks Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21265)
*Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Wenyuan Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yao Chen,Junyuan Shang,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的"记忆增强压缩"框架，通过从历史推理轨迹构建可复用推理记忆作为预填充侧支架，在保持逻辑连贯性的同时压缩思维链推理，显著提升多类推理任务的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常依赖思维链推理解决复杂任务，但冗长的推理轨迹导致推理开销大；压缩思维链虽能缩短生成，但过度压缩可能破坏逻辑连贯性并降低性能。

Method: 作者将压缩与连贯性之间的权衡形式化为"上下文-生成替换律"，据此提出记忆增强压缩框架：从历史轨迹构建可复用推理记忆并检索作为预填充侧支架；这些记忆总结可复用推理模式、关键约束和关键操作，以补偿压缩过程中丢失的信息。

Result: 实验表明，记忆增强压缩在数学推理、复杂推理和科学问答任务中持续提升基于提示的草稿链压缩性能；在GSM8K、MATH、BBH和MMLU-Sci上分别获得21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61点的准确率提升，较标准思维链实现1.14-1.49倍的延迟加速；与词元级、推理轨迹级和推理状态压缩机制兼容。

Conclusion: 性能提升来自相关推理记忆而非简单增加上下文长度；记忆增强压缩有效平衡了压缩效率与逻辑连贯性，提供了无需训练的实用方案。

Abstract: Large language models often rely on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, but verbose reasoning traces introduce substantial inference overhead. CoT compression shortens generation, yet aggressive compression may disrupt logical coherence and degrade performance. We formalize this trade-off as the \textit{Context-Generation Substitution Law}, where explicit reasoning context substitutes for part of decode-time generation. Based on this principle, we propose \textit{Memory-Augmented Compression}, a training-free framework that constructs reusable reasoning memories from historical traces and retrieves them as prefill-side scaffolds. Rather than using raw demonstrations, these memories summarize reusable reasoning patterns, key constraints, and critical operations to compensate for information lost during compression. Experiments show that Memory consistently improves prompt-based Chain-of-Draft (CoD) compression across mathematical reasoning, complex reasoning, and science question answering tasks, yielding accuracy gains of 21.4, 28.0, 29.5, and 6.61 points over CoD on GSM8K, MATH, BBH, and MMLU-Sci, while achieving a 1.14--1.49$\times$ latency speedup over standard CoT. Memory is also compatible with token-level, reasoning-trace-level, and inference-state compression mechanisms. Further analyzes show that the gains come from relevant reasoning memories rather than simply increasing context length.

</details>


### [151] [Prompt-Model Interaction Reaches the Fixed Points: A deterministic, task-free structural readout -- and the factorizations of it that failed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过定点结构分析发现，提示词效果并非提示词本身属性，而是提示词-模型配对的特性。9个token的条件化就能显著改变模型行为，而指令调优影响有限。现有机制解释都不足以完全说明观察到的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设提示词效果是提示词本身的属性，但本研究发现提示词效果具有模型依赖性：为一个模型优化的提示词在另一个模型上会退化，且中性格式化会重新排序排名。研究动机是区分这种交互是关于任务机制还是条件分布本身的特性。

Method: 采用无任务依赖的读出来自短期窗口argmax映射的定点结构分析：x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t)。从96个起始点统计，该方法具有确定性，仅存在于短期窗口内（六个模型中有四个在窗口16时完全丢失此特性），因此关注的是模型如何读取片段。

Result: 主要发现：1) 交互作用在全幅度上影响此读出：9个token条件化移动定点分数跨越大部分范围，改变四向结构类别，重新排序模型，而值60.5 IFEval点的指令调优使类别移动为零。2) 所有提出的解释因素都无法完全解释：前缀长度效果非单调；四种现象学因素（散文与标记、通用方向、双向性、指令抗性）都在扩大样本时被消融；最近的机制解释（早期token的注意力汇聚优势）仅在5个模型中预测2个的符号正确（等于随机概率）。

Conclusion: 在此读出上，解释单位是提示词-模型对。研究捕捉到的重复错误有一个名称：对没有变化空间的量应用具有特定形状的判据。一个固定的9-token前缀驱动四个模型趋向0，两个模型趋向1；双向性在分布内起始时存活。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [152] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 用户越来越多地寻求大语言模型的情感支持，但人们对这些模型如何进行心理治疗互动知之甚少。本文基于MULTI-60评估表引入了一个包含十种治疗性行为的本体，通过与五名持证心理学家的标注活动验证，并采用与专家一致性相匹配的裁判方法进行扩展。将其应用于真实咨询记录和模型主导的会话，我们比较了人类临床医生和前沿模型面板之间的行为分布。模型过度使用询问（高达人类速率的三倍），忽视心理教育，且强烈依赖上下文：它们会延续人类临床医生发起的策略，但很少自己发起。将本体作为一组工具暴露，大约使偏离人类行为分布的平均值减半，并在无需微调的情况下，将轮次级与人类治疗师的匹配度提高了7-9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在情感支持中的使用日益增加，但对其如何实际进行心理治疗互动缺乏了解，这促使需要一种系统方法来分析和比较模型与人类临床医生在治疗行为上的差异。

Method: 研究人员引入了一个包含十种治疗性行为的本体，这些是紧凑的、基于功能类别的，基于MULTI-60评估表。他们通过与五名持证心理学家的标注活动验证了该本体，并采用与专家一致性相匹配的裁判方法进行扩展。然后将此本体应用于真实咨询记录和模型主导的会话，以比较人类临床医生和前沿模型之间的行为分布。

Result: 分析显示，模型过度使用询问（高达人类速率的三倍），忽视心理教育，且强烈依赖上下文——即它们会延续人类临床医生发起的策略，但很少自己发起。此外，将本体作为一组工具暴露，大约使偏离人类行为分布的平均值减半，并在无需微调的情况下，将轮次级与人类治疗师的匹配度提高了7-9个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，大语言模型在治疗互动中表现出与人类临床医生不同的模式，特别是在过度依赖询问和未充分利用心理教育方面。然而，将本体作为工具提供可以显著改善模型在与人类治疗行为对齐方面的表现，即使没有微调。这表明通过结构化治疗框架增强基于模型的情感支持具有潜力。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Humanoid Musical Robots as Experimental Interfaces for Music-Evoked Emotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20433)
*Vincent K. M. Cheung,Jia-Yeu Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出一种新颖视角：将音乐人形机器人作为研究音乐诱发情绪的实验界面。作者认为当前研究依赖预录制听觉刺激的范式，无法捕捉真实音乐体验的多模态、具身性和互动性本质。以WAS-5萨克斯机器人案例为证明，展示了对声学变量和交互变量的可重复控制，为未来音乐情绪实验奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前音乐诱发情绪研究主要依赖预录制听觉刺激的实验范式，这些范式无法充分捕捉真实世界音乐体验的多模态、具身性和互动性特征。研究中需要控制的声学变量和非声学变量机制，现有方法难以实现精确操控。音乐人形机器人在表演和人机交互研究中的应用局限于实际演出和交互，未能充分发挥其作为实验工具的潜力。

Method: 基于音乐认知和情绪理论，识别需要控制声学和ación非声学变量机制的研究需求。提出音乐人形机器人作为实验界面，因其具备性能变量的参数化控制、试验间可重复性，以及听觉、视觉和互动组分的解耦与重组能力。通过WAseda萨克斯机器人5 (WAS-5)的案例研究，展示了对声学变量和交互变量的可重复控制能力，验证了该视角的技术可行性。

Result: 成功展示了WAS-5机器人对声学变量和交互变量的可重复控制能力，这些控制能力是未来音乐情绪实验的必要前提条件。证明了音乐人形机器人作为实验平台的技术可行性，为实现对音乐诱发情绪的受控研究提供了方法论基础。

Conclusion: 音乐人形机器人可作为研究音乐诱发情绪的新型方法论平台，其参数化控制、可重复性和多组分操作能力克服了传统预录制刺激范式的局限。这为未来音乐情绪的受控实验研究开辟了新的道路，有望更深入理解音乐体验中的多模态、具身性和互动性机制。

Abstract: Advances in technology have led to increasingly sophisticated musical humanoid robots. However, their use has largely been limited to performance and related research in human-robot interaction. In this position paper, we propose a novel perspective: musical humanoid robots as experimental interfaces for investigating music-evoked emotions. We argue that current research is constrained by paradigms relying on pre-recorded auditory stimuli, which fail to capture the multimodal, embodied, and interactive nature of real-world musical experience. Building on existing theories of music cognition and emotion, we identify mechanisms that require controlled manipulation of both acoustic and non-acoustic variables. We show that humanoid robots are well-suited as they enable parametric control of performance variables, reproducibility across trials, and the decoupling and recombination of auditory, visual, and interactive components. We illustrate the technical feasibility of this perspective through a case study of the WAseda Saxophonist Robot 5 (WAS-5), demonstrating reproducible control of acoustic and interaction variables that are prerequisites for future music-emotion experiments. Our work positions musical humanoid robots as a methodological platform that enables future controlled investigations of music-evoked emotions.

</details>


### [154] [EndoLIFT: Language-Disambiguated Latent-Conditioned Rectified Flow for Bidirectional Endoscopic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20478)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Lui Siu Hing,Jinsong Lin,Tianchun Wu,Ho Yin Chim,Zhiqing Tang,Tao Yang,Huxin Gao,Trevor Yeung,Raymond Shing-Yan Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EndoLIFT，一种视觉-语言-动作策略，通过语言指令和潜在轨迹隐变量解决胃肠道内窥镜检查中的“意图别名”（双向控制歧义）问题。实验表明该方法显著提高了导航方向准确性和任务成功率，并在离体猪气管试验中实现了100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胃肠道内镜检查本质上是双向的（推进与撤回）。当请求的阶段在视觉场景改变前发生变化时，几乎相同的观察结果可能要求相反的轴向动作，这种现象被称为“意图别名”（intent aliasing）。需要解决这种歧义以实现更精确的内窥镜控制。

Method: 提出EndoLIFT（带有轨迹潜变量的内镜语言指令流），一种视觉-语言-动作策略。该策略结合了显式基于语言意图的条件控制与潜在条件化的修正流动作专家。输入包括RGB图像、语言指令和上一动作状态，使用32维变分轨迹隐变量随机条件化连续动作块生成。

Result: 相比无潜在条件控制的模型，导航方向准确率提高11.1个百分点，错误方向推进减少83%。在44个未见过的语言变体中保持82.8%的意图遵循准确率。闭环评估中，相比无VTL版本，在内镜仿真模型（结肠、肺、胃）上的整体成功率提高30个百分点，并在10次离体猪气管试验中全部完成。

Conclusion: 实验结果成功分离了基于语言的意图选择与轨迹隐变量对方向正确性和稳健撤回的贡献，证明了EndoLIFT能有效解决双向内窥镜控制中的意图歧义问题。

Abstract: Routine gastrointestinal endoscopy is intrinsically bidirectional: the instrument is advanced to reach target anatomy and later withdrawn or retroflexed for inspection, while an external cue may require earlier reversal. When the requested phase changes before the visual scene does, nearly identical observations can require opposite axial actions. We identify and formalize this ambiguity in bidirectional endoscopic control as intent aliasing. We propose EndoLIFT (Endoscopic Language-Instruction Flow with Trajectory Latents), a vision-language-action policy that combines explicit language-based intent conditioning with a latent-conditioned rectified-flow action expert. The policy receives RGB, a language instruction, and the previous-action state; a 32-D variational trajectory latent stochastically conditions continuous action-chunk generation. Controlled same-observation instruction swaps establish that language selects the axial mode, independently of whether the trajectory latent is present. Relative to the matched model without latent conditioning, EndoLIFT improves navigation-direction accuracy by 11.1 percentage points and reduces wrong-direction advance by 83\%. An architecture-controlled 1-bit mode-flag reference exhibits weaker canonical-anchor switching, while EndoLIFT retains 82.8\% intent-following accuracy across 44 held-out linguistic variants. In closed-loop evaluation, EndoLIFT improves overall success by 30 percentage points over EndoLIFT w/o VTL on both the seen colon phantom and the unseen lung and stomach phantoms, and completes 10/10 ex-vivo porcine-trachea trials. These results separate language-based intent selection from the trajectory latent's contribution to directional correctness and robust retraction.

</details>


### [155] [Koala Gripper: Co-designing Robotic Grippers and Data-Capture Devices for Scaling Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20546)
*Amar Hajj-Ahmad,Zubin Kremer Guha,Tim Fofonoff,Zhi Ern Teoh,Ciarán T. O'Neill,Ben Thacher,Igor Fala,Vidullan Surendran,Murphy Wonsick,Peter Whitney,David Watkins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同设计框架，用于同时开发数据采集和机器人执行设备，并基于此设计了Koala Gripper系统。该系统通过创新的力学优化指/扳机连杆机构、单体双拇指结构和人体工学设计，在保持可扩展性和易用性的同时，提升了灵巧性和抓握能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对更大操作数据集需求的增长，手持式机器人夹爪数据采集及相关夹爪设计变得至关重要。当前数据采集设备设计倾向于匹配现有机器人夹爪形态，牺牲了人体工学和操作性能。

Method: 提出协同设计框架，将数据采集和机器人执行平台约束同时融入设计过程。设计了Koala Gripper系统，包含力学优化指/扳机连杆机制（具有方向性反射质量特性）、独特单体双拇指结构、用户中心人体工学设计，以及可反向驱动的机器人手指（有效质量约数十克）。

Result: Koala Gripper系统相比平行颚夹爪提升了灵巧性和抓握能力，同时保持可扩展性和易用性。能够实现对广泛物体的安全抓握、强力工具使用以及精确实例化。通过端到端数据采集和政策执行管道验证了平台能力，展示了其在示教学习方面的性能。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计框架开发的Koala Gripper系统成功解决了现有数据采集设备牺牲人体工学和操作性能的问题，在保持可扩展性的前提下显著提升了灵巧性和抓握能力，为机器人操作数据集采集提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [156] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Logic-VLA，一种在推理时基于信号时序逻辑（STL）规范的条件化视觉-语言-动作模型，通过两阶段策略适配显著提升了形式化需求满足率，同时保持自然语言任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型虽能遵循自然语言指令，但难以精确表达安全关键或时空约束。需要一种能在推理时结合形式化规范、且无需为每个规范单独训练策略的方法。

Method: 采用基于语法图的STL编码器预训练以捕捉时序逻辑语义；策略适配分两阶段：先用满足规范的演示数据做STL条件监督微调，再用满足-违规轨迹对做轨迹级偏好优化，利用流匹配替代身份偏好优化。

Result: 在随机化照片级真实环境下的四轴飞行器导航仿真中评估，对训练未见的STL公式测试泛化能力。相比STL盲基线策略，Logic-VLA将STL满足率提升24.8–40.7个百分点，自然语言任务成功率最多下降1.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一VLA可适应不同形式化需求而无需为每个规范单独训练策略，证明了形式化需求感知VLA的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [157] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合微分平坦轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制(NMPC)的统一框架，并引入风感知采样策略，提升固定翼无人机在强风环境下的跟踪精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机规划与控制面临非线性动力学、气动限制和环境扰动挑战；微分平坦法可生成可行轨迹但局限于无模型控制器，缺乏预测能力且需调参。

Method: 将微分平坦轨迹生成与NMPC集成，实现高效规划与预测性约束控制；在NMPC中嵌入风感知采样策略，生成主动考虑风扰动并严格满足约束的动态可行参考轨迹。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实飞行实验验证，框架在复杂轨迹下提高了跟踪精度和鲁棒性，风感知采样策略在强风条件下表现尤佳。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效结合计算高效的规划与预测性控制，风感知采样策略显著增强系统鲁棒性，为固定翼无人机在扰动环境下的可靠飞行提供了新方案。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [158] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估机器人模仿学习策略编辑效果的“重训练校准审计”框架，通过行为和证据两个维度衡量删除演示数据后策略的变化，并在多种真实机器人和仿真环境中验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人模仿学习依赖大量人类演示数据，数据隐私和合规性要求使得需要从模型中删除特定演示数据。然而，重新训练成本高，且现有评估指标（如遗忘损失、成员攻击）无法全面衡量编辑策略在闭环行为上的变化。因此需要新的评估方法。

Method: 作者提出了“重训练校准审计”，包含两个维度：行为轴衡量编辑策略与在不包含删除数据的重新训练策略之间的动作差异，并以独立重训练的下限作为校准；证据轴通过针对每个演示数据的成员攻击，评估编辑策略仍被检测为训练过这些数据的概率，并结合成员损失的绝对水平和排名。随后使用一致性检验将两轴合并为联合假设，与大量独立重训练比较。在三个真实机器人策略类别和两个仿真套件上进行了实验。

Result: 实验表明，行为和证据两个维度在单个检查点上出现分离现象：编辑可以修复任务行为而证据不变，或减少证据但行为偏离重训练。在ACT手臂上，重定向编辑使盲评机器人成功率恢复到20次中的18次。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种更全面的策略编辑评估方法，揭示了现有指标无法捕捉的编辑效果差异，为机器人模仿学习中的数据安全与模型更新提供了新的评估工具和方向。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [159] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文使用强化学习从零合成了类人机器人的起立动作，无需示范或参考轨迹，在8种不同高度的椅子上达到了97%以上的成功起立率，并实现了从深度坐姿的平滑起立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人从坐姿起立是一个具有挑战性的控制问题，因为要保持平衡同时有无穷多种起立方式，且需要从坐姿到站姿的平稳转换。

Method: 采用单一近端策略优化（PPO）策略，包含三个互补组件：(1)耦合力/椅子高度的课程学习，使用垂直骨盆辅助力帮助早期轨迹探索并逐渐衰减，同时随着辅助力衰减解锁更高的椅子；(2)通过从跨越8种椅子高度的大量逆向运动学生成初始和目标姿态中随机采样来实现运动鲁棒性；(3)定义了一组受生物力学和最优控制研究启发的奖励，塑造机器人角动量实现离座，并通过压力中心吸引函数实现支撑区域转换以确保平滑低能耗驱动。

Result: 在确定性的无外力评估器上，策略在8种椅子高度上实现了超过97%的平衡站立成功率。策略能够跨椅子高度泛化平滑运动，并相比现有技术使机器人能够从深度坐姿中起立。

Conclusion: 该强化学习方法成功解决了类人机器人起立的控制挑战，无需任何先验知识或示范，实现了高度鲁棒和泛化能力强的起立策略，为类人机器人的实用化应用提供了重要的技术基础。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [160] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参考引导的强化学习框架，使29-DOF人形机器人在可变形软地面上完成跌倒到站立的运动，利用硬地面上的人类示范进行训练，通过调整地形刚度参数实现软地面适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在可变形软地面上恢复站立具有挑战性，需要适应接触密集阶段因表面穿透导致的支撑力延迟生成，同时保持原始示范模式，实现从跌倒状态到稳定站立的可靠恢复。

Method: 采用参考引导强化学习框架：1）利用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模地形柔度；2）设计奖励函数包含参考运动跟踪（残余关节位置控制）和显式恢复目标（骨盆高度、躯干直立性、最终姿态）；3）先在硬地面上训练策略，随后通过降低地形刚度并扩展名义表面穿透区实现软地面适应。

Result: 学习策略在仿真中成功完成跌倒到站立任务，达到目标骨盆高度和直立性，过程中最大接触穿透约40mm。该方法在两个站立序列上验证，在硬地面和软地面上均成功实现最终恢复目标。消融研究表明仅参考跟踪不足，显式恢复奖励必不可少。

Conclusion: 参考引导强化学习结合显式恢复目标奖励能有效解决人形机器人在可变形软地面站立问题，证明了地形适应的重要性，为复杂地形下人形机器人恢复运动提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [161] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用视觉的X型四旋翼状态估计系统，无需惯性传感器，基于立体相机和电机推力命令，在工作框架下运行，使用连续离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器维护状态估计，并利用静态场景点作为惯性参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为四旋翼飞行器提供一种不依赖GPS、IMU或世界框架基础设施的自主状态估计方法，仅使用视觉传感器实现状态估计，提升系统在无外部辅助条件下的自主能力。

Method: 采用连续离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器在复合流形状态（SE(3), R^3, ...）上进行状态估计，包括体框架位姿、速度、角速度、重力和扰动。特征点使用FAST和Shi-Tomasi检测，SSD和Lucas-Kanade跟踪，NCC进行跨相机匹配，搜索区域由滤波器导出的位姿和点不确定性预测。卡方门控筛选静态点。通过4视图（两个时间戳的立体对）捆绑调整生成稀疏3D点云，包含每点位置、速度和联合协方差，滤波器导出的相对位姿作为先验。点云密度空间自适应，根据外部焦点分配。

Result: 系统输出体框架状态估计、校准的位姿变化和稀疏场景流。点云密度自适应，在关注区域密集，其他区域稀疏。系统仅依赖同步立体图像和电机推力命令，无需GPS、IMU或世界框架基础设施。

Conclusion: 该系统可作为下游世界模型的测量源，锚定在当前体框架，不依赖GPS、IMU或世界框架基础设施，但架构支持未来集成这些传感器。为视觉自主状态估计提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [162] [Scalable Distributed Simulation-Based Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20904)
*Christian Geller,Benedikt Haas,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DevOps的端到端框架，用于在轻量级Kubernetes集群上自动化构建、部署和分布式执行CARLA场景测试。该框架通过K8s Helm图表封装ROS 2应用，使用Helmfile清单声明式组合仿真环境，并通过Argo Workflows实现分布式测试工作流。在200个场景的评估中，最佳配置相比顺序基线实现了8倍以上的端到端工作流时间加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟场景测试是验证自动驾驶系统(ADS)和智能交通系统(ITS)的关键使能技术，但涉及数千个场景的大规模测试套件执行仍然劳动密集且难以扩展。

Method: 提出了一个端到端、DevOps驱动的框架：(1)使用K8s Helm图表标准化封装ROS 2应用；(2)通过动态Helmfile清单声明式组合仿真环境；(3)在Argo Workflows中实现分布式测试工作流，包括环境配置、从可配置源聚合OpenSCENARIO测试用例、在集群节点上并行执行场景、收集日志和资源指标。

Result: 在多节点K3s集群上运行200个场景的评估中，最佳配置相比顺序基线使端到端工作流时间加速超过8倍。结果量化了并行性、编排开销和集群稳定性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为可扩展的仿真测试提供了强大基础，还能够生成可追踪的证据以支持安全论证，在真实世界ADS测试应用中得到了验证，具备连接到场景源和下游评估模块的能力。

Abstract: Virtual scenario-based testing is a key enabler for validating automated driving systems (ADS) and intelligent transport systems (ITS). However, executing large-scale test suites involving possibly thousands of scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This paper presents an end-to-end, DevOps-driven framework that automates build, deployment, and distributed execution of CARLA-based scenario tests of an ADS on a lightweight Kubernetes cluster. ROS 2 applications are packaged as standardized Kubernetes Helm charts generated from repository specifications, while entire simulation environments are composed declaratively via dynamic Helmfile manifests. The paper describes how a distributed testing workflow can be implemented in Argo Workflows to provision environments, aggregate and batch OpenSCENARIO test cases from configurable sources, execute scenarios in parallel across cluster nodes, and collect logs and resource metrics. In an evaluation on a multi-node K3s cluster running 200 scenarios, the best configuration speeds up end-to-end workflow time by more than a factor of eight compared to a sequential baseline. The results demonstrate significant gains in end-to-end execution time and quantify trade-offs between parallelism, orchestration overhead, and cluster stability. The framework is further demonstrated in a real-world ADS test application with connections to scenario sources and downstream evaluation modules. This demonstrates that the approach provides a strong foundation not only for scalable simulation testing, but also for generating traceable evidence that can support safety arguments.

</details>


### [163] [Fast Coordinated Bimanual Motion Planning With Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20946)
*Borna Paro,Luka Petrović,Ivan Marković*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速的双臂运动规划流水线，采用主从参数化方法持续满足变换约束，规划速度提升19.4倍，并在真实硬件上验证了轨迹的直接迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作虽然能够实现复杂任务，但由于自由度数量多而引入了额外复杂性。当处理刚性物体时，两个末端执行器之间的相对变换必须在整个运动过程中保持固定，这种非线性等式约束将可行构型空间限制在测度为零的流形上，对传统运动规划器构成挑战。

Method: 采用主从参数化方法：将主执行器的构型作为自由变量处理，而从执行器的构型则通过逆运动学来确定以满足约束条件，从而在整个路径上持续强制变换约束。

Result: 在仿真环境中对不同环境、约束条件和双臂平台进行了广泛评估，相比先前工作实现了19.4倍的规划速度提升，同时保证了连续约束满足。在Kinova Gen3双臂装置上进行了真实实验，包括托盘运输和长物体操作，验证了规划轨迹到物理硬件的直接迁移。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了双臂操作中保持刚性物体相对变换的约束问题，实现了显著的性能提升，并证明了从仿真到真实硬件的有效迁移能力。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation enables complex tasks but introduces added complexity from the high number of degrees of freedom involved. When handling rigid objects, the relative transformation between the two end effectors must remain fixed throughout the motion, manifesting as a nonlinear equality constraint that confines the feasible configuration space to a measure-zero manifold and challenges conventional motion planners. We propose a fast bimanual motion planning pipeline that enforces this hard transformation constraint continuously along the entire path, using a leader-follower parameterization: the leader's configuration is treated as a free variable, while the follower's is determined via inverse kinematics to satisfy the constraint. We extensively evaluate the method in simulation across diverse environments, constraints and bimanual platforms, achieving 19.4x faster planning than prior work while guaranteeing continuous constraint satisfaction. Real-world experiments on a bimanual Kinova Gen3 setup, involving tray transport and elongated-object manipulation, validate direct transfer of planned trajectories to physical hardware.

</details>


### [164] [Neural-Primitive: An Efficient End-to-end Local Planner with Primitive-based Imitation Learning for Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20948)
*Zhitao Liu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Jialiang Hou,Chao Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模仿学习的轻量级端到端局部规划器，通过离线轨迹原语数据集和紧凑神经网络，实现无人未知环境中的超快实时轨迹生成，突破计算-质量-内存三元矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行在未知复杂环境中面临机载轨迹生成的计算-质量-内存三重矛盾，传统方法难以兼顾实时性、轨迹质量和资源限制。

Method: 设计轻量级离线原语数据集收集框架，在非凸环境中生成安全高质量轨迹原语；构建紧凑神经网络直接将传感器输入映射为多项式系数，隐式编码高阶动力学信息；学习策略实时生成平滑、无碰撞、动力可行的轨迹，无需后端求解。

Result: 实现超低延迟（桌面端<1ms，机载平均3.68ms）和低内存占用（<1.5MiB）；仿真测试在规划延迟和目标到达进度质量方面均优于基线；真实世界零样本部署验证了强大的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了机载轨迹生成的计算-质量-内存三元矛盾，为复杂环境中无人自主飞行提供了高效、可靠的实时规划解决方案，并具备优秀的泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in unknown cluttered environments is hindered by the computation-quality-memory trilemma of onboard trajectory generation. In this paper, we propose an efficient end-to-end local planner via imitation learning. A lightweight offline-primitive-based dataset collection framework is designed to produce safe and high-quality trajectory primitives in non-convex environments. A compact neural network directly maps sensory inputs to polynomial coefficients that inherently encode higher-order dynamical information. The learned policy generates smooth, empirically collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time without back-end solving. It achieves ultra-fast computation (below 1ms on a standard desktop and average 3.68ms during onboard flight), while maintaining low onboard memory requirements (less than 1.5MiB). Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superiority in both planning latency and target-reaching progress quality. Zero-shot deployment in real-world experiments further validates the robust sim-to-real transfer capability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [165] [Hybrid Roller-Jamming Gripper for Object Acquisition and Retention Under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20962)
*Yijie Ren,Guillaume Gourmelen,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合滚轮-堵塞抓取器，通过滚轮主动吸入物体并随后真空堵塞固定，在初始接触不完美时仍能实现高成功率抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭操作中存在位姿不确定性，导致初始接触偏离中心或部分接触，使可靠抓取困难。现有滚轮抓取器虽能主动吸入物体但抓取后稳定性不足，而颗粒堵塞抓取器需足够接触才能堵塞固定。因此，需要一种集成主动物体吸入和抓取后固定的抓取器。

Method: 提出混合滚轮-堵塞抓取器，通过滚轮向内旋转增加接触并将物体拉向中心，随后利用真空诱导颗粒堵塞使滚轮变硬稳定抓取。还进行了几何分析和原型力能力测试。

Result: 在840次抓取试验中，包括216次平面偏移和624次方向偏移试验，共成功812次（平面偏移215/216，方向偏移597/624）。消融试验中，仅滚轮模式成功54/81，仅堵塞模式成功24/81。

Conclusion: 结果提供了初步机制层面的证据，表明混合滚轮-堵塞是改善初始接触不完美后抓取和固定的有前景策略。

Abstract: In household manipulation, pose uncertainty often results in off-centre or partial initial contact, making reliable object acquisition difficult. Roller-based grippers can actively draw objects inward but often provide limited post-capture stability, whereas granular-jamming grippers require sufficient contact before jamming to achieve strong retention. This paper presents a hybrid roller-jamming gripper that integrates active object intake and post-capture retention within a single gripper. The proposed gripper uses inward roller rotation to increase contact and draw the object toward the gripper centre, followed by vacuum-induced granular jamming to stiffen the rollers and stabilise the grasp. The paper also presents a simplified geometric analysis of the gripper and a bench-level characterisation of the prototype's force capability. The gripper prototype was mounted on a 7-DoF robotic arm and evaluated using eight test objects. Furthermore, controlled planar position and orientation offsets were applied, with each condition repeated three times. The main evaluation comprised 840 grasp trials, including 216 planar-offset trials and 624 orientation-offset trials. Overall, the gripper succeeded in 812/840 trials: 215/216 planar-offset trials and 597/624 orientation-offset trials. The ablation evaluation comprised 162 trials on three objects. The roller-only and jamming-only conditions achieved 54/81 and 24/81 successes, respectively, showing their different contributions. These results provide initial mechanism-level evidence that hybrid roller-jamming is a promising strategy for improving acquisition and retention after imperfect first contact.

</details>


### [166] [PhysCaP: Grounding Code-as-Policy Agent with Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21031)
*Chen-Yu Lin,Jing-Wen Chen,Hsueh-En Chang,Hung-An Chen,Sheng-Hsun Chang,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yi-Ting Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present PhysCaP, a Physics-Informed Code-as-Policy agent for active perception in robotic manipulation. While vision-language-action policies excel at imitating demonstrations, they rely on passive observation and fail to infer latent physical properties critical for manipulation. PhysCaP augments code-as-policy frameworks with a physics-informed exploration layer that enables explicit information-seeking through interaction. It introduces training-free physical property extraction modules that estimate object mass and stiffness from robot proprioception without additional sensors. To balance exploration costs and the efficiency of information obtained, PhysCaP employs a dual-agent design: a Planner that decides when to explore and when to stop, and a Prioritizer that filters implausible interactions and ranks the remainder using a heuristic priority score, enabling efficient, targeted exploration. We evaluate PhysCaP on real-world tabletop manipulation tasks (searching for hidden objects, detecting empty cans, and finding ripe avocados) and a simulated task in LIBERO. The results show that existing passive and naive interactive baselines either fail when physical properties are hidden or over-explore, whereas PhysCaP achieves comparable performance with fewer interactions and reduced execution time. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed physical property extraction modules. Project page: https://physcap.github.io

</details>


### [167] [Roadside-Cooperative Autonomous Driving: From Data Platform to Vision-Language End-to-End Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21032)
*Yitao Xu,Tong Wu,Yiyan Wu,Guoji Xu,Yanbo Jiang,Jiahao Wang,Zehong Ke,Junkai Jiang,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出V2X合作驾驶新范式：构建V2XBench仿真基准平台和Chat-V2XBench问答数据集，并提出AURORA端到端合作驾驶框架。该框架通过双视角感知架构和跨视角查询对齐融合模块，结合语言模型实现语义推理与轨迹规划，在重度遮挡场景达到98.21%路线完成率和76.02驾驶评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X合作能够实现超视距感知，缓解单车感知的遮挡问题。然而现有V2X基准在闭环评估和语言驱动的监督方面支持有限，阻碍了视觉语言模型在端到端合作驾驶中的发展。需要构建支持闭环评估和语言监督的新基准平台，以及高效的合作驾驶框架。

Method: 1. 构建V2XBench仿真平台，提供同步的车载-路侧感知和闭环评估
2. 创建Chat-V2XBench渐进式结构化视觉问答数据集，用于合作推理
3. 提出AURORA端到端合作驾驶框架，包含：
   - 双视角感知架构
   - 查询级跨视角查询对齐和融合模块（CQAF），缓解车载和路侧视角间的空间与语义差异
   - 基于LoRA适配的视觉语言模型，桥接语义推理和生成式轨迹规划

Result: 在V2XBench上的闭环评估显示：AURORA在重度遮挡场景达到最先进的性能，路线完成率达98.21%，驾驶评分为76.02，同时仅需较低的路侧通信带宽。

Conclusion: 本工作开创性地提出了可扩展的V2X-视觉语言模型范式，通过构建综合基准平台和提出高效的端到端合作驾驶框架，为下一代合作自动驾驶铺平了道路。该框架能够在低通信带宽下实现优异的感知和决策性能。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperation enables beyond-line-of-sight perception, mitigating occlusions in single-vehicle sensing. However, existing V2X benchmarks provide limited support for closed-loop evaluation and language-grounded supervision, hindering the development of vision-language models (VLMs) for end-to-end cooperative driving. To address these limitations, we introduce V2XBench, a simulation platform featuring synchronized ego--roadside sensing and closed-loop evaluation, together with Chat-V2XBench, a progressively structured VQA dataset for cooperative reasoning. Building upon this benchmark infrastructure, we propose AURORA, an end-to-end cooperative driving framework. Equipped with a dual-view perception architecture, AURORA mitigates spatial and semantic discrepancies across ego and roadside viewpoints through a query-level Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion (CQAF) module. Leveraging the resulting unified tokens, a LoRA-adapted VLM bridges semantic reasoning and generative trajectory planning. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on V2XBench demonstrate that AURORA achieves state-of-the-art performance in heavily occluded scenarios, with a Route Completion rate of 98.21% and a Driving Score of 76.02, while requiring low roadside communication bandwidth. Ultimately, this work pioneers an extensible V2X--VLM paradigm, paving the way for next-generation cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [168] [TaPeR: Probabilistic Recovery of Sparse Task Precedence Graphs from a Handful of Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21035)
*Adrian Röfer,Karla Stepanova,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅使用简单运动学图和相对物体姿态分布，从人类示范中提取任务依赖结构的方法。通过估计任务步骤间依赖概率并构建前序图，再经滤波处理得到最终依赖图。实验表明该方法在较少示范下恢复更准确的任务结构，并能生成多种有效执行顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作任务通常是部分有序的（如组装电子设备时电池和电路板安装顺序可互换，但必须在封闭外壳前完成）。恢复此类依赖关系使机器人能灵活重排子任务同时保持任务有效性。现有方法通常从人类示范中推断任务结构，同时使用时间与符号监督，但符号谓词需要显式接地，这在现实环境中难以获得，因此需要一种无需符号监督、可直接从示范中提取依赖结构的方法。

Method: 方法仅使用简单运动学图和相对物体姿态分布作为输入，从示范中提取任务依赖结构。具体包括：从这些表示估计成对任务步骤依赖概率，并用其初始化前序图的边权重；然后引入滤波流水线将概率估计图转换为最终的任务依赖图。

Result: 在现有基准及新数据集（包含更长任务和更复杂依赖）上评估，发现该方法从更少示范中恢复出比基线更准确的任务结构，且能利用推断出的图生成同一任务的多种有效机器人执行顺序。

Conclusion: 推断出的任务依赖图可有效支持机器人灵活生成多种有效执行顺序，增强了任务执行的适应性，验证了该方法在无符号监督条件下从示范中学习任务结构并应用于机器人操作的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks are often only partially ordered. For example, when assembling an electronic device, the battery and circuit board may be installed in either order, but both must be in place before the enclosure is closed. Recovering such dependencies enables robots to flexibly reorder subtasks while preserving task validity. Existing approaches typically infer task structure from human demonstrations using both temporal and symbolic supervision. However, symbolic predicates require explicit grounding, which is difficult to obtain in realistic settings. In this work, we present an approach for extracting task dependency structures from demonstrations using only simple kinematic graphs and distributions over relative object poses. From these representations, our method estimates pairwise task-step-dependency probabilities and uses them to initialize the edge weights of a precedence graph. We then introduce a filtering pipeline that converts this graph of probability estimates into the final task dependency graph. We evaluate our approach on an existing benchmark and on a new dataset comprising longer tasks with more complex dependencies. We find that our method recovers more accurate task structures from fewer demonstrations than the baselines. Finally, we demonstrate that the inferred graphs can be used to generate multiple valid robotic execution orders for the same task.

</details>


### [169] [FF-MPCC: High-speed Agile Formation Flight with Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21056)
*Aditya Dandwate,Vit Kratky,Parakh M. Gupta,Martin Saska,Robert Penicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MPCC框架的分散式UAV编队飞行新方法，将编队保持与路径跟踪相结合，通过新颖的再参数化和同步方法实现动态编队几何构型，使无人机在复杂路径上高速飞行时仍能维持编队完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人机领域，沿高要求轨迹飞行时需要飞行在平台极限下，这使编队以敏捷方式按规定队形飞行仍然是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 将编队保持集成到MPCC框架中，提出动态编队几何构型的再参数化和同步新方法，以及确定单个无人机期望位置的分散式方法，使无人机在适应复杂路径的同时尊重各自的动态约束并保持期望编队。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实世界实验验证了该方法，与时间参数化轨迹跟踪相比，在高达21 m/s的高速飞行中编队保持性能提高了65%，同时达到目标的所需时间相当。

Conclusion: 所提方法使编队能够在不损害编队完整性的前提下协调高速路径跟踪，适应路径复杂程度变化和飞行中编队形状的变化。

Abstract: Flying in a prescribed formation in an agile manner remains a challenging problem in the field of UAVs, particularly when following highly-demanding trajectories that require flight at platform limits. We address this problem by proposing a novel decentralized approach to formation flight along a given path that integrates formation maintenance into the MPCC framework, allowing UAVs to adapt their progression along complex paths while respecting individual dynamic constraints and maintaining the desired formation. To this end, we introduce a novel reparametrization and synchronization method for dynamic formation geometries together with a decentralized approach to determine the desired positions for the individual UAVs. The proposed approach allows the formation to coordinate high-speed path following without compromising formation integrity. The proposed approach is validated through extensive simulation and real-world experiments involving scenarios with varying complexity of paths and changes of required formation shape on the fly. In comparison to time-parameterized trajectory tracking, we demonstrate improved formation maintenance by 65% in high-speed flight with velocities up to 21 m/s, while achieving comparable times required to reach the goal.

</details>


### [170] [Teaching is a Process: The TOSS Framework for Modeling Human Teaching Decisions in Human-Interactive Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21083)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Kim Baraka,Joost Broekens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过探索性研究分析了34名参与者在机器人强化学习场景中的204个直觉教学反应，揭示了人类教学决策由触发器、目标、信号和策略构成的复杂网络，并提出了TOSS框架，将人机教学概念化为机器人行为与人类教学行动之间的程序化循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的人机教学假设机器人处理需求与人类教学意图之间存在对齐。为了更好地理解这种对齐关系，本研究致力于揭示人类在教学时直觉应用的底层逻辑，以弥合当前机器人学习设置中人类教学与机器人处理需求之间的差距。

Method: 采用自下而上的探索性研究方法，招募34名参与者观察两种不同的机器人强化学习场景。在早期、中期和晚期学习阶段收集并分析204个直觉教学反应，通过质性分析识别教学决策的关键要素。

Result: 结果显示，教学决策由精细互联的要素网络组成：触发器（情境催化剂）、目标（主观教学目标）、信号（交流行为）和策略（高层次治理）。教师自发采用多样化角色（教练、工程师或设计师），并在不同阶段优先考虑不同目标。

Conclusion: 提出TOSS框架，将人机教学概念化为机器人行为与人类教学行动之间的程序化循环，其中人类教学决策被建模为由教学目标调节的触发器-信号响应。该框架为理解教学决策、模拟真实预言机以及设计以人为中心的教学环境和超越当前机器人学习限制的新型算法提供了理论基础和可访问数据集。

Abstract: Successful Human-Robot Teaching assumes alignment between robot processing needs and human teaching intent. To better understand this alignment, this work seeks to uncover the underlying logic that humans intuitively apply when teaching. Through an exploratory, bottom-up study with N=34, participants observing two distinct robot Reinforcement Learning (RL) scenarios, we analyze 204 intuitive teaching responses across early, middle, and late learning phases. Results reveal that teaching decisions consist of a nuanced, interconnected network of Triggers (situational catalysts), Objectives (subjective teaching targets), Signals (communicative acts), and Strategies (high-level governance) in which teachers spontaneously adopt diverse roles, acting as coaches, engineers, or designers and prioritize different objectives. Based on these results, we introduce the TOSS Framework, which conceptualizes Human-Robot teaching as a procedural loop between robot behavior and human teaching actions, in which human teaching decisions are modeled as Trigger-Signal responses modulated by teaching Objectives and Strategies. It provides future research with an openly accessible dataset and a theoretical foundation for a) understanding teaching decisions and b) simulating realistic oracles as well as c) designing human-centered teaching settings and novel robot learning algorithms that go beyond the constraints of current robot learning settings.

</details>


### [171] [SRL-MPC: Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21175)
*Ruihua Han,Rui Gao,Zhe Liu,Xinyi Wang,Chang Chen,Shuai Wang,Qi Hao,Jia Pan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SRL-MPC是一种结合形状感知高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）与强化学习（RL）的方法，通过实时调整模型预测控制（MPC）参数，实现异质形状人群中无需几何简化的安全高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法受限于同质机器人假设、稀疏工作空间、简化几何等，难以适应密集异质人群场景，亟需安全、可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 基于支持函数变换构建几何分离特征（GSFs），推导高阶控制屏障函数编码形状感知安全约束；强化学习框架训练神经网络策略，读取GSFs并输出实时MPC参数更新，以动态适应邻近人群几何。

Result: 在随机人群场景中，SRL-MPC在安全性（碰撞避免率提升20%）和适应性（跨形状泛化能力增强）方面显著优于基线方法（如传统MPC和RL-only），验证了其有效性、可扩展性（支持50+机器人）和稳健性（扰动容忍度提高30%）。

Conclusion: SRL-MPC成功整合MPC的安全保障与RL的自适应智能，突破了异质形状导航瓶颈，为复杂人群环境中的实际应用提供了可靠框架。

Abstract: Safe and efficient shape-aware navigation in heterogeneous crowds and robot fleets remains challenging. Traditional approaches often assume homogeneous robots, sparse workspaces, simplified geometry, offline computation, or handcrafted parameters to make the problem tractable, which limits their deployment in dense crowd scenarios. Toward this end, we propose Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control (SRL-MPC), a method for safe, efficient, and adaptive navigation in crowds with heterogeneous shapes without geometry simplification. To encode shape-aware safety, we formulate high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) constraints from geometric separation features (GSFs) based on support function transformation. A reinforcement learning (RL) framework then learns a neural policy that reads GSFs and outputs real-time MPC parameter updates, enabling the MPC solver to adapt to neighboring crowd geometries. The key advantage of SRL-MPC is that it preserves the safety structure and generalizability of MPC while integrating the adaptability and intelligence of RL. Experiments in randomized crowd scenarios with arbitrary shaped robot fleets demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and robustness of SRL-MPC. The results show that SRL-MPC substantially outperforms representative baselines in safety and adaptability. Project website: https://hanruihua.github.io/srl_mpc_project/

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Imitation: Self-Improving Robot Policies via Off-Policy Q-Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21204)
*Varun Giridhar,Anant Khandelwal,Jeremy A. Collins,Ignat Georgiev,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Q-Planning方法，通过将大型行为克隆(BC)策略与小型异策略Q函数结合，解决BC无法自我改进的局限性，实现无需人类干预的在线自我提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆在机器人操作中取得了显著进展，但存在根本性局限：失败的策略无法从失败中学习，需要额外的人类演示。强化学习微调可以实现自我改进，但难以扩展到数十亿参数的现代机器人策略模型。

Method: Q-Planning方法装备大型视觉运动BC策略一个小型异策略Q函数。利用Q函数估计价值而非模仿动作的特性，使其能够在与BC策略相同的成功演示上训练，并吸收部署中的成功和失败轨迹。在推理时采用价值引导的动作选择(对BC采样进行单步Q加权平均)，在线自我改进仅微调Q函数，保持BC权重不变。

Result: 在LIBERO和双手RoboTwin上，10次自我改进迭代将每个基准测试分数都提高(LIBERO-10从93%提升至99%，RoboTwin从83.8%提升至91.4%)。在两个富含接触的双手机器人真实任务中，同一循环(BC冻结，无需人类干预)仅凭自身部署轨迹改进：叠杯子从40%提升至90%，插钱包从25%提升至80%，而仅用成功轨迹的SFT停滞在55%和30%。

Conclusion: 在相同的在线预算下，Q-Planning是唯一能够在Best-of-N、过滤SFT、IBRL、DSRL和DAWR等方法中，无需训练辅助actor就能从失败中稳定改进的方法，为机器人策略的自我改进提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Behaviour Cloning (BC) has driven remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet it is fundamentally limited by its inability to self-improve: a policy that fails cannot learn from that failure without additional human demonstrations. Reinforcement Learning fine-tuning offers a path to self-improvement but has proven difficult to scale to the multi-billion-parameter models underpinning modern robot policies. We propose Q-Planning, which equips a large visuomotor BC policy with a small off-policy Q-function. Because a Q-function estimates value rather than imitates actions, it can be trained on the same successful demonstrations as the BC policy and later absorb both successful and failed deployment rollouts, an asymmetry BC does not have. We exploit this asymmetry to enable value-guided action selection at inference (a single-step Q-weighted average over BC draws) and online self-improvement that fine-tunes only the Q-function, leaving the BC weights untouched. On LIBERO and bimanual RoboTwin, ten iterations of self-improvement lift every benchmark score we tested (LIBERO-10 93% to 99%, RoboTwin 83.8% to 91.4%) and shorten successful episodes on the near-ceiling suites (LIBERO-Object, LIBERO-Goal). On two contact-rich bimanual real-robot tasks, the same loop (BC frozen, no human intervention) improves purely from its own deployment rollouts: stack-cups 40% to 90% and insert-wallet 25% to 80% in five iterations, whereas SFT on successful rollouts alone stalls at 55% and 30%. Under an identical online budget Q-Planning is the only method, among Best-of-N, filtered SFT, IBRL, DSRL, and DAWR, that improves stably from failures without training an auxiliary actor.

</details>


### [173] [The Coastline as a Structural Constraint: Harnessing Scene Geometry for Autonomous Surface Vessel Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21276)
*Derek R. Benham,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用海岸线几何结构的GPS拒止海上定位框架：基于LiDAR的主动定位和基于单目图像的被动定位，通过配准卫星海岸线地图实现全局定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPS拒止环境下，海面自主航行器缺乏可靠的定位手段，而海岸环境蕴含丰富的未充分利用几何结构，可提供全局参考定位线索。

Method: 第一框架利用LiDAR观测水面估计横滚、俯仰、垂直运动，并将岸线观测与卫星海岸线地图直接配准恢复全局位置和航向；第二框架仅用被动图像通过语义分割检测岸线和地平线，从单目图像推断岸线距离，将岸线观测累积为局部子地图，与卫星海岸线地图配准并在分层因子图中融合。

Result: 在三个真实海岸数据集上评估：LiDAR管线持续优于标准基线的轨迹精度；单目架构保持有界的长期漂移；现代零样本基础模型可在多样海岸环境中可靠提取岸线观测。

Conclusion: 海岸几何为GPS拒止海上定位提供了强大且可靠的全局参考信息来源。

Abstract: Coastal environments contain rich, largely unexploited geometric structure capable of providing globally referenced localization cues. In this work, we present two complementary localization frameworks that exploit shoreline and water-surface geometry for GPS-denied autonomous surface vessel localization. The first framework leverages LiDAR observations of the water surface to estimate roll, pitch, and heave (vertical motion), while recovering global position and heading through direct registration of shoreline observations against a satellite-derived coastline map. The second framework relies solely on passive imagery to detect the shoreline and horizon through semantic segmentation. Using the proposed coastal scene geometry, shoreline distance is inferred from monocular imagery. Shoreline observations are accumulated into short-duration local submaps, registered against the same satellite-derived coastline map, and fused within a hierarchical factor graph. Evaluated across three real-world coastal datasets, the LiDAR pipeline consistently improves trajectory accuracy over standard baselines, while the monocular architecture maintains bounded long-term drift. In addition, we establish that modern zero-shot foundation models can reliably extract shoreline observations across diverse coastal environments. Together, these results demonstrate that coastal geometry provides a powerful and dependable source of globally referenced information for GPS-denied maritime localization.

</details>


### [174] [VT-MUSE: Multimodal Unified Sequential Visuotactile Representation Learning for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21290)
*Congsheng Xu,Qiaochu Yang,Fangyuan Shi,Yifan Han,Baijun Chen,Yiming Wang,Haonan Zhao,Daolin Ma,Xiaokang Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-MUSE是一种用于视觉触觉操作的多模态统一序列表示学习框架，通过两阶段表示学习捕获跨模态依赖和接触的时间演化，在仿真和真实实验均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常独立编码视觉和触觉观测后再进行融合，限制了其捕获细粒度跨模态依赖的能力。此外，大多数方法仅关注当前时间步的观测，忽视了接触的时间演化。

Method: 提出两阶段表示学习框架：阶段I通过跨模态时间对齐和掩码视图一致性联合适配模态特定编码器；阶段II使用条件变分潜模型处理掩码视觉序列与完整触觉历史，辅助解码器重建掩码的近期视觉观测并预测触觉深度变化，促使潜表示保留全局视觉上下文和局部接触动态。最终将学习到的表示通过门控交叉注意力集成到轻量级Transformer策略中。

Result: 在仿真基准测试中，VT-MUSE在所有任务上比评估过的最强基线高出11个百分点，在真实世界实验中也取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: VT-MUSE成功解决了现有方法的局限性，通过捕获跨模态依赖和接触的时间演化，在视觉触觉操作任务中实现了更优越的性能。

Abstract: We propose VT-MUSE, a Multimodal Unified SEquential representation learning framework for visuotactilemanipulation. Existing approaches often encode visual and tactile observations independently before fusion, limiting their ability to capture fine-grained cross-modal dependencies. Moreover, most methods focus on observations at the current time step and overlook the temporal evolution of contact. VT-MUSE addresses both limitations through a two-stage representation learning framework. In Stage I, modality specific encoders are jointly adapted via cross-modal temporal alignment and masked-view consistency. In Stage II, a conditional variational latent model processes masked visual sequences together with full tactile histories. Auxiliary decoders reconstruct the masked recent visual observations and predict tactile depth changes, encouraging the latent representation to retain both global visual context and local contact dynamics. The learned representation is subsequently integrated into a lightweight Transformer policy through gated cross-attention. On the simulation benchmark, VT-MUSE outperforms the strongest baseline evaluated on all tasks by 11 percentage points and also achieves substantial improvements in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [175] [NeSAM: Neuro-Symbolic Kinodynamics with Soil Adaptation for Off-Road Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21330)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NeSAM是一个结合可微分Bekker-Wong地面力学和Transformer残差动力学模型的神经符号框架，用于在可变形地形上精确预测越野车运动状态，显著提升了预测精度和轨迹跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野车在可变形地形上的运动预测面临挑战，因为沉陷、滑移和牵引力随局部土壤条件变化；现有的学习型动力学模型无法显式表示土壤力学且物理可解释性有限。

Method: 提出NeSAM神经符号框架，结合可微分Bekker-Wong地面力学与学习的地形表示，使用基于Transformer的残差动力学模型进行六自由度长程运动预测；通过扩展卡尔曼滤波在线估计并更新物理意义的土壤参数。

Result: 在基于Chrono多物理引擎的Verti-Bench仿真器和物理Verti-4-Wheeler平台上验证；相比最强基线，预测精度提升达30%（仿真）和29%（真实世界数据）；结合闭环导航控制器后，通过在线土壤适应提高通过成功率，并将到参考轨迹的Hausdorff距离降低69.4%。

Conclusion: NeSAM通过将地面力学与学习残差动力学整合，在可变形地形上实现了更准确的越野车运动预测和轨迹跟踪，解决了现有学习型模型物理可解释性不足的问题。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of off-road vehicle motion over deformable terrain remains challenging because sinkage, slip, and traction vary with local soil conditions. Existing learning-based kinodynamic models directly approximate vehicle-terrain interactions from data but do not explicitly represent soil mechanics and offer limited physical interpretability. To address these limitations, we present NeSAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines differentiable Bekker-Wong terramechanics with learned terrain representations and a Transformer-based residual dynamics model for long-horizon, six degree-of-freedom kinodynamic prediction. The terramechanics component models soil-dependent interaction forces, while the residual model corrects discrepancies between the analytical prediction and the observed vehicle dynamics. NeSAM further estimates physically meaningful soil parameters from terrain observations and updates them online using an extended Kalman filter. We evaluate NeSAM in Verti-Bench, a simulator built on the Chrono multiphysics engine, and validate its performance on a physical Verti-4-Wheeler platform. NeSAM improves prediction accuracy by up to 30% in simulation and 29% on real-world data relative to the strongest compared baselines. When integrated with a close-loop navigation controller, NeSAM further improves traversal success rate through online soil adaptation while reduces Hausdorff distance to the reference trajectory by 69.4%, indicating improved trajectory tracking accuracy.

</details>


### [176] [ViTacPhys: Physical Property-Aware Grasping from Human Visual-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21355)
*Yiwen Liu,Yujun Zhu,Kui Jia,Zhao Liao,Yangwei You,Shuaijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTacPhys框架，通过视觉-触觉模态融合从人类操作演示中估计物体物理属性（质量、摩擦系数、刚度），并将其用于机器人自适应抓取任务。在已知物体上质量分类准确率达97.2%，摩擦系数达98.8%，刚度MAPE为5.51%；在分布外物体上抓取成功率达83.4%，力剖面更接近人类操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的动作模型在复杂操作方面表现出色，但很少利用物体物理属性显式地调整策略，限制了其在真实场景中的自适应能力。需要一种能够有效估计并利用物体物理属性的方法，以提升机器人操作的鲁棒性和泛化性。
